To address this issue, we propose normalized entropy, which measures confidence deviations relative to an agent's average behavior under a given state, thereby strengthening the association between low-quality actions and trajectory neglect. Building on this insight, we introduce Selective Trajectory-Aware Policy Optimization (STAPO), a hierarchical group-based RL framework. STAPO leverages normalized entropy to locate outlier steps associated with trajectory neglect and optimizes them via a joint mechanism of trajectory-aware reward and trajectory-independent penalty, enhancing trajectory awareness while preserving training stability. Extensive experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-Augmented QA demonstrate that STAPO achieves state-of-the-art performance while substantially alleviating trajectory neglect, validating its effectiveness and robustness for agentic tasks.

</details>


### [184] [Quantum-Inspired Harmonic Decision Models: A Computational Framework for Music Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05007)
*Josef Pavlíček,Petra Pavlíčková,Martin Molhanec*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子启发式音乐和声决策框架，将和声编排建模为结构化组合空间中的优化问题，结合量子干涉搜索与经典优化，在流行歌曲上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子启发方法在音乐和声生成中的应用，将和声决策形式化为受约束组合空间中的优化问题，模拟人类作曲中并行考虑多种和声可能性的认知过程。

Method: 提出一个两阶段框架：(1) 量子启发干涉阶段，并行评估多个候选和弦序列；(2) 经典优化阶段，基于调性和声理论进行细化，确保结构连贯性和风格合理性。

Result: 在Autumn Leaves等作品上验证，量化分析显示优化阶段显著降低和弦密度、提高和声稳定性并改善功能组织；专家评估表明和声复杂性增加并不总被视为更自然，强调了风格语境的重要性。

Conclusion: 量子启发方法可为音乐等创意领域的复杂决策过程提供有用建模框架，和声生成可被解释为受约束搜索空间中的结构化决策过程。

Abstract: This paper introduces a quantum-inspired computational framework for harmonic decision-making in music. The proposed approach formulates harmonization as an optimization problem within a structured combinatorial space, where multiple candidate chord sequences are evaluated under interacting musical constraints. The model combines an interference-based harmonization stage with a classical optimization procedure grounded in tonal harmony. The quantum-inspired component enables the parallel consideration of multiple harmonic alternatives, while the classical stage refines the resulting sequences to ensure structural coherence and stylistic plausibility.
  The framework is evaluated on selected musical examples, including Autumn Leaves and It's a Long Way to Tipperary. Quantitative analysis shows that the optimization stage significantly reduces chord density, increases harmonic stability, and improves functional organization. At the same time, expert evaluation highlights the importance of stylistic context, demonstrating that increased harmonic complexity is not always perceived as more natural. The results suggest that harmonic generation can be interpreted as a structured decision-making process in a constrained search space. The proposed approach provides a computational model that integrates domain-specific knowledge with an interference-based search mechanism.
  Although preliminary, this work indicates that quantum-inspired methods may offer a useful framework for modeling complex decision processes in creative domains such as music. The proposed framework contributes to ongoing research on quantum-inspired models of cognition and decision-making in complex biological and creative systems.

</details>


### [185] [Toward Trustworthy Large Language Model Agents in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05055)
*Hadi Hasan,Safaa Salman,Adam Tai Abou Dargham,Ammar Mohanna,Ali Chehab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CareConnect是一个基于大语言模型的安全优先对话代理系统，通过函数调用、检索增强生成和分层确定性安全护栏，实现医疗预约调度自动化。在680个任务场景的基准测试中，任务完成率达91.8%，中位延迟2.2秒，安全合规率96.0%，每次预约成本仅0.0324美元，显著低于人工调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗预约调度长期受限于手动协调、碎片化遗留系统和高管理负担，导致运营效率低下，制约了医疗资源可及性和患者就诊体验。

Method: 采用LLM函数调用、RAG和分层确定性安全护栏，编排8个领域特定工具支持预约全流程操作，通过紧急检测短路机制和医疗意图拒绝机制强制执行安全范围约束。

Result: 680个任务场景中任务完成率91.8%，中位延迟2.2秒，安全关键子集合规率96.0%，每次预约平均成本0.0324美元。

Conclusion: 精心限定范围并严格保护的LLM代理能够可靠地自动化复杂医疗运营流程，同时保持安全保证并实现显著的成本效益。

Abstract: Healthcare appointment scheduling remains a persistent operational bottleneck, driven by manual coordination, fragmented legacy systems, and high administrative overhead. These inefficiencies constrain provider availability and degrade patient access to care. This paper presents CareConnect, a safety-first conversational agent for healthcare logistics automation that leverages large language model (LLM) function calling, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and layered deterministic safety guardrails. The system orchestrates eight domain-specific tools to support appointment booking, modification, cancellation, and facility information retrieval, while enforcing strict scope constraints that prohibit medical advice or diagnosis. Safety-critical situations are handled through deterministic short-circuit mechanisms for emergency detection and medical intent refusal. We evaluate CareConnect on a comprehensive benchmark of 680 task-oriented scenarios spanning end-to-end workflows, multi-turn interactions, and edge cases. Experimental results demonstrate a 91.8% task completion rate with a median per-request latency of 2.2 seconds, 96.0% safety compliance on the dedicated safety-critical evaluation subset, and an average operational cost of $0.0324 per appointment, yielding a significant cost reduction compared to manual human scheduling. These findings show that carefully scoped and rigorously safeguarded LLM-based agents can reliably automate complex healthcare operational workflows while maintaining safety guarantees and achieving substantial cost efficiency. The source code and system implementation are publicly available at https://github.com/Hadi-Hsn/CareConnect.

</details>


### [186] [Diffusion-Guided Uncertainty-Aware Delayed Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05064)
*Junqi Tu,Zejiao Liu,Fangfei Li,Yang Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对强化学习中延迟反馈导致的性能下降问题，本文理论证明了随机MDP下延迟状态与真实状态之间存在固有差异，并提出Diffusion Guided Uncertainty Aware Delayed Policy Optimization (DUPO)方法，利用扩散模型建模延迟状态与当前状态的关系，通过差异估计加权延迟策略，在连续机器人控制任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实环境中的强化学习常因反馈延迟导致性能严重下降，现有方法通过构建增广状态或预测真实状态来缓解，但忽略了随机MDP下延迟状态与真实状态之间的固有差异，该差异会导致最优策略退化。

Method: 提出DUPO方法：1）利用扩散模型显式建模延迟状态信息与当前状态之间的关系；2）将由此产生的差异估计用于加权延迟策略，以校正延迟带来的偏差。

Result: 在多个具有随机延迟的连续机器人控制任务上，DUPO方法一致优于现有方法，即使在长延迟和随机延迟场景下仍能保持有效性。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上证明了随机MDP中延迟状态与真实状态的固有差异及其对最优策略的负面影响，并提出了有效的DUPO方法，为延迟强化学习提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning in real world environments often suffers from severe performance degradation due to delayed feedback. Existing approaches typically mitigate performance degradation caused by observation delays by constructing augmented states or predicting the true states. However, these methods often overlook the inherent discrepancy between delayed state and true states induced by stochastic MDP. We theoretically prove the existence of such a discrepancy and show that it leads to the degradation of the optimal policy. To address this challenge, we propose Diffusion Guided Uncertainty Aware Delayed Policy Optimization (DUPO). Our method explicitly models the relationship between delayed state message and the current state using a diffusion model, and leverages the resulting discrepancy estimates to weight delayed policies. Extensive experiments on continuous robotic control tasks with multiple stochastic delays demonstrate that DUPO consistently outperforms existing methods and remains effective even under long and random delay scenarios.

</details>


### [187] [ASSEMCAD: Production-Ready CAD Assembly Generation from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05123)
*Yurui Dong,Shu Zou,Siqi Li,Nianchen Deng,Hongbin Zhou,Xuemeng Yang,Pinlong Cai,Licheng Wen,Xinyu Cai,Botian Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AssemCAD提出了一种基于公理框架的文本到CAD装配生成方法，通过构建包含类型化零件、几何端口、可执行配合和工程公理的装配规范，结合端口-配合CAD库实现确定性几何执行，显著提升了装配的物理有效性和可复用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-CAD方法仅能生成单个零件，无法处理多组件协调、功能接口、装配关系和工程原理约束下的机械装配生成问题，直接生成CAD代码难以保证装配的机械有效性和可复用性。

Method: AssemCAD首先构建公理性的装配规范（Assembly Specification），包含类型化零件、几何端口、可执行配合和工程公理；然后引入基于端口和配合的CAD装配库，通过确定性配合变换执行符号装配关系，并用B-Rep几何证据验证接口；最后支持按需合成可复用的参数化组件工厂。

Result: 在AssemBench上的实验表明，AssemCAD在装配保持和物理有效性上显著优于基于代码的CAD生成基线，并能泛化到不同的基础模型骨干网络上。

Conclusion: 通过将公理基础的装配推理与确定性几何执行相结合，AssemCAD将Text-to-CAD从孤立零件生成扩展到面向生产就绪的机械装配设计。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models and programmatic CAD have significantly improved Text-to-CAD generation for individual parts. However, production-ready mechanical assembly generation remains largely unsolved. Unlike single-part modeling, assemblies require coordinated reasoning over multiple components, functional interfaces, assembly relations, engineering principles, and physical consistency. Consequently, directly generating executable CAD code is insufficient for constructing mechanically valid and reusable assemblies. We present AssemCAD, an axiom-grounded framework for production-ready CAD assembly generation from natural language. Instead of representing an assembly as monolithic CAD code, AssemCAD first constructs an axiomatic Assembly Specification consisting of typed parts, geometry-backed ports, executable mates, and engineering axioms. Each assembly relation is explicitly grounded in one or more engineering principles, making the resulting specification interpretable, reusable, and verifiable. To realize this specification, AssemCAD introduces a port- and mate-based CAD assembly library that executes symbolic assembly relations through deterministic mate transformations and validates declared interfaces using concrete B-Rep geometric evidence. Built on this representation and library, AssemCAD further supports on-demand synthesis of reusable parametric component factories for both standard and open-world geometries. Experiments on AssemBench show that AssemCAD substantially improves assembly preservation and physical validity over code-centric CAD generation baselines, while generalizing across different foundation-model backbones. By combining axiom-grounded assembly reasoning with deterministic geometric execution, AssemCAD extends Text-to-CAD from isolated part generation toward production-ready mechanical assembly design.

</details>


### [188] [TacReasoner: A Dynamic Tactile-Language Framework for Interactive Reasoning in Real-World Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05131)
*Kailin Lyu,Di Wu,Long Xiao,Jianning Zeng,Jianwei He,Chang Lin,Lianyu Hu,Lin Shu,Jie Hao,Ce Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TacReasoner框架，通过动态感知触觉编码器和触觉思维链数据集TouchCoT-10k，解决动态触觉信号建模不足和触觉基础模型幻觉问题，在多项任务中以7B参数超越14B模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉对多模态推理至关重要，但现有研究面临两大挑战：(i)动态触觉信号建模不足，限制了对时间演化属性的推理；(ii)触觉基础模型缺乏显式推理机制导致幻觉，影响真实世界推理的稳定性。

Method: (1) 提出TacReasoner框架，包含动态感知触觉编码器增强动态触觉信号感知与表示；(2) 构建TouchCoT-10k，首个触觉思维链数据集，用于结构化触觉推理；(3) 建立DynTac-Bench基准，系统评估动态触觉感知和真实世界常识推理。

Result: TacReasoner在多个数据集上达到与最先进模型竞争的性性能，且仅用7B参数即在大多数子任务上超越14B的VTV-LLM模型，展示了在触觉常识推理中的高效性和有效性。

Conclusion: 通过动态感知编码器和触觉思维链推理，TacReasoner有效解决了动态触觉建模和幻觉问题，为触觉多模态推理提供了高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Among the five primary human senses, tactile is arguably the most fundamental to survival, as it enables the perception of physical contact and interaction in real-world environments. In this paper, we explore two key challenges of integrating tactile sensing into intelligent systems for multimodal reasoning: (i) insufficient modeling of dynamic tactile signals, which restricts reasoning over temporally evolving properties, and (ii) hallucination in tactile foundation models caused by the absence of explicit reasoning mechanisms, leading to unstable real-world inference. To address these challenges, we propose TacReasoner, a dynamic tactile-language framework for interactive reasoning in real-world scenarios. First, TacReasoner incorporates a Dynamic-aware Tactile Encoder to enhance the perception and representation of dynamic tactile signals. More importantly, we introduce TouchCoT-10k, the first tactile chain-of-thought dataset for structured reasoning over tactile inputs. Upon it, we establish DynTac-Bench to systematically evaluate dynamic tactile perception and real-world commonsense reasoning. Experimental results demonstrate that TacReasoner achieves competitive performance against state-of-the-art models across multiple datasets. Notably, despite using only 7B parameters, TacReasoner outperforms the 14B VTV-LLM model on most subtasks, highlighting its effectiveness and efficiency in tactile commonsense reasoning.

</details>


### [189] [DSpark: Confidence-Scheduled Speculative Decoding with Semi-Autoregressive Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05147)
*Xin Cheng,Xingkai Yu,Chenze Shao,Jiashi Li,Yunfan Xiong,Yi Qian,Jiaqi Zhu,Shirong Ma,Xiaokang Zhang,Jiasheng Ye,Qinyu Chen,Chengqi Deng,Jiping Yu,Damai Dai,Zhengyan Zhang,Yixuan Wei,Yixuan Tan,Wenkai Yang,Runxin Xu,Yu Wu,Zhean Xu,Xuanyu Wang,Muyang Chen,Rui Tian,Xiao Bi,Zhewen Hao,Shaoyuan Chen,Huanqi Cao,Wentao Zhang,Anyi Xu,Huishuai Zhang,Dongyan Zhao,Wenfeng Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DSpark是一种推测解码框架，通过半自回归架构和自适应负载感知验证策略，在保持高吞吐量的同时提升生成质量和系统效率，相比生产基线MTP-1在匹配吞吐量下实现了60-85%的每用户生成速度提升，并拓展了服务系统的帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并行推测解码器虽能单次前向传播生成较长token序列，但缺乏token间依赖导致接受率快速衰减；同时，无差别验证长块浪费批处理容量在高拒绝风险的token上，严重降低高并发服务系统的吞吐量。

Method: DSpark采用半自回归架构，将并行主干与轻量级顺序模块耦合，引入块内依赖建模以减轻后缀衰减；同时采用置信度调度验证机制，基于估计的前缀存活概率和引擎特定吞吐量特征动态调整每个请求的验证长度。

Result: 在跨域离线基准测试中，DSpark相较最先进的自回归和并行推测解码器显著提升了接受长度；在DeepSeek-V4服务系统实际流量部署中，相比MTP-1基线，匹配吞吐量下每用户生成速度提升60-85%，并防止了严格交互约束下的吞吐量严重下降，实现了此前不可达的性能层级。

Conclusion: DSpark通过统一高吞吐并行生成与自适应负载感知验证，有效解决了并行推测解码中的接受率衰减和服务效率问题，推动了服务系统帕累托前沿的移动。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates Large Language Model (LLM) inference by decoupling draft generation from target verification. While recent parallel drafters efficiently propose long token sequences in a single forward pass, they suffer from rapid acceptance decay due to a lack of inter-token dependencies. Furthermore, indiscriminately verifying these extended blocks wastes critical batch capacity on tokens with high rejection risks, severely degrading throughput in high-concurrency serving systems. We introduce DSpark, a speculative decoding framework that unifies high-throughput parallel generation with adaptive, load-aware verification. To maintain draft quality, DSpark utilizes a semi-autoregressive architecture, coupling a parallel backbone with a lightweight sequential module, to introduce intra-block dependency modeling and mitigate suffix decay. To optimize system efficiency, DSpark employs confidence-scheduled verification, dynamically tailoring the verification length for each request based on estimated prefix survival probabilities and engine-specific throughput profiles. On offline benchmarks across diverse domains, DSpark substantially improves the accepted length over state-of-the-art autoregressive and parallel drafters. When deployed within the DeepSeek-V4 serving system under live user traffic, DSpark successfully mitigates verification waste. Compared to the established production baseline (MTP-1), DSpark accelerates per-user generation speeds by 60 to 85 percent at matched throughput levels. More importantly, by preventing severe throughput degradation under strict interactivity constraints, it enables performance tiers that were previously unattainable, shifting the Pareto frontier of our serving system.

</details>


### [190] [The Changing Role of Symbolic Methods in Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05168)
*Jun Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出压缩原理：显式符号推理并非智能的本质属性，而是计算模型简化现实的信息补偿机制。由此推导出建模-推理权衡：模型保留的信息越丰富，对符号推理的需求越低。同时，随着AI系统能力增强和黑箱化，符号方法反而对人类更重要，应作为人与AI系统之间的接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基础模型在极少显式符号推理的情况下取得巨大成功，挑战了传统认知科学将符号推理视为智能核心成分的观点，亟需重新审视符号方法在AI中的角色定位。

Method: 提出压缩原则作为核心理论框架，从信息论角度将计算模型视为现实的简化表征，并推导出建模-推理权衡关系，以此统一解释符号方法的成功与基础模型的有效性。

Result: 证明了符号推理的需求与模型信息保留程度呈反比关系；揭示了AI系统越强大越需要符号方法作为人机接口这一"悖论性"结论。

Conclusion: 符号方法的未来不在于作为智能系统的计算引擎，而应转向充当人类与日益强大的AI系统之间的符号化接口，用于需求规范、行为验证、自主系统管制和信任建立。

Abstract: Why do intelligent systems need to perform explicit symbolic reasoning? Computer science has traditionally regarded symbolic reasoning as a defining component of intelligence. Yet the remarkable success of modern foundation models raises a fundamental question: if increasingly capable AI systems can operate with little explicit symbolic reasoning, what role do symbolic methods actually play?
  This article argues that explicit symbolic reasoning is not a fundamental property of intelligence, but a computational consequence of operating on simplified models of reality. We propose the Compression Principle: every computational model is a simplified representation of reality, and explicit symbolic reasoning compensates for information omitted during model construction. From this principle, we derive the Modeling--Reasoning Trade-off: as computational models preserve richer representations of the world, the need for explicit symbolic reasoning correspondingly decreases. This perspective provides a unified explanation for both the historical success of symbolic methods and the remarkable effectiveness of modern foundation models.
  Paradoxically, the same development makes symbolic methods increasingly important for humans. As intelligent systems become more capable and more opaque, symbolic representations increasingly serve as interfaces through which humans specify requirements, verify behavior, regulate autonomous systems, and establish trust. We therefore argue that the future of symbolic methods lies not primarily as the computational engine of intelligent systems, but as the symbolic interface between increasingly capable AI systems and the humans who build, govern, and depend upon them.

</details>


### [191] [AgentGym2: Benchmarking Large Language Model Agents in De-Idealized Real-World Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05174)
*Zhiheng Xi,Dingwen Yang,Jiaqi Liu,Jixuan Huang,Honglin Guo,Baodai Huang,Tinggang Chen,Qi Zhang,Zhonghang Lu,Chenyu Liu,Jiajun Sun,Jiazheng Zhang,Dingwei Zhu,Xin Guo,Junzhe Wang,Zhihao Zhang,Yuming Yang,Junjie Ye,Minghe Gao,Dongrui Liu,Jiaming Ji,Guohao Li,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentGym2是一个新的评估框架，通过基于真实端到端工作需求的任务实例，衡量语言智能体在探索工具、组合工具、执行完整流程以及应对噪声和不完整信息方面的能力。实验表明，包括Gemini和GPT-5在内的最先进系统在该基准上表现不佳，揭示了当前智能体能力与现实应用需求之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试在简化、理想化环境下评估智能体，低估了真实部署中存在的噪声、不确定性和主动探索需求，亟需更贴近现实的评估框架。

Method: 提出AgentGym2评估框架，基于真实世界端到端工作需求构建任务实例，衡量智能体在推理规划之外的执行完整流程、探索发现工具、组合工具处理未见任务、以及对噪声和未指定信息的鲁棒性等能力。

Result: 在15个专有和开源模型上的实验显示，包括Gemini和GPT-5在内的最先进系统在AgentGym2上表现不佳。

Conclusion: 当前智能体能力与现实应用需求之间存在实质性差距。

Abstract: Language agents, i.e., LLM agents, progress rapidly and are increasingly deployed in production environments. This trend underscores the urgent need for rigorous and realistic evaluations. However, most existing benchmarks evaluate agents in simplified, idealized settings. They typically rely on pre-packaged tool interfaces, overlook critical steps, and assume inputs are clean and fully specified. Consequently, they understate the difficulty of real deployments, where uncertainty and noise are ubiquitous and agents must proactively explore the environment to uncover new tools. To bridge this gap, we present AgentGym2, a new evaluation framework with task instances grounded in real-world end-to-end working demands. Beyond reasoning and planning, it measures agents' ability to execute end-to-end procedures, discover tools via exploration, compose tools for unseen tasks, and remain robust to noisy and underspecified information. Experiments on 15 proprietary and open-source models show that even SOTA systems like Gemini and GPT-5 struggle on AgentGym2, revealing a substantial gap between the capability of current agents and the demands of real-world applications.

</details>


### [192] [CP-WSP: A Declarative CP-SAT Framework for Configurable Multi-Constraint Workforce Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05177)
*Vipul Patel,Anirudh Deodhar,Dagnachew Birru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CP-WSP框架，通过CP-SAT求解器强制执行14个硬约束并优化15个软目标，支持班次窗口变量分解、带中点控制的强制休息调度、 acuity加权工作量公平性、多粒度时间分辨率、跨周排班稳定性以及跨午夜班次处理，在INRC-II基准和36个合成配置上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CP公式通常以班次粒度建模简化实例（6-12个约束），缺乏对强制休息调度（含中点位置控制）、acuity加权工作量公平性、子班次时间粒度、跨周排班稳定性以及24小时运营中常见的跨午夜班次模式的显式支持。

Method: 提出CP-WSP声明式CP-SAT框架，包括：班次窗口变量分解实现带中点控制的强制休息调度；acuity加权工作量公平性；30分钟至2小时的多粒度时间分辨率；跨周排班稳定性；网格偏移预处理技术处理跨午夜班次；通过统一加权惩罚函数优化15个软目标；全部通过JSON规范配置无需代码修改。

Result: 在INRC-II基准（小时级和班次级粒度）以及36个合成配置上进行了评估。

Conclusion: CP-WSP提供了比现有CP公式更全面的约束覆盖（14个硬约束+15个软目标），通过可复现的基准套件支持社区比较，且配置灵活无需代码修改。

Abstract: Workforce scheduling is an NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem requiring simultaneous satisfaction of labor regulations, coverage requirements, employee preferences and operational objectives. Existing CP formulations typically model simplified instances with 6-12 constraints at shift-level granularity and critically lack explicit support for: mandatory break scheduling with midpoint placement control; acuity weighted workload equity; sub-shift temporal granularity enabling demand-driven staffing; inter-week schedule stability; and cross-midnight shift patterns common in 24-hour operations. This paper presents CP-WSP: a declarative CP-SAT framework enforcing 14 hard constraints as mathematically inviolable requirements (zero regulatory violations by construction) while optimizing 15 soft objectives through a unified weighted penalty function -- all configurable via a JSON specification with no code changes required. Key contributions include: a shift-window variable decomposition enabling mandatory break scheduling with centrality control; acuity-weighted workload equity; multi-granularity temporal resolution from 30 minutes to 2 hours; inter-week schedule stability; a grid-offset preprocessing technique for cross-midnight shifts; and a reproducible 36-configuration benchmark suite for community comparison. Evaluated on INRC-II benchmarks at both hourly and shift-level granularity and on 36 synthetic configurations.

</details>


### [193] [Rethinking On-Policy Self-Distillation for Thinking Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05184)
*Simran Kaur,Narutatsu Ri,Yinghui He,Liam Fowl,Sanjeev Arora*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现，在思维模型（thinking models）中使用特权上下文进行自我蒸馏（privileged self-distillation）会导致长推理轨迹性能下降，最高相对降幅达17%，原因是特权上下文改变了高熵分支位置的学习信号，抑制了模型在推理过程中的验证、回溯和修正行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我蒸馏（self-distillation）是语言模型自我改进的有前景方法，即模型在有特权信息（如数学题解）时充当自己的教师。这对思维模型（thinking models）尤其有吸引力，因为它们可以利用测试时推理来吸收特权信息。然而，作者发现一个反直觉现象：特权自我蒸馏反而会损害思维模型在长推理轨迹上的表现。

Method: 作者在五种Qwen3和OLMo思维模型上，在AIME24、AIME25和HMMT25三个基准上评估特权上下文蒸馏效果；对比on-policy蒸馏（OPD）与特权OPD；分析推理轨迹中的分叉率（fork rate）、自纠错分支、验证/回溯/对冲标记等token级信号。

Result: 特权上下文蒸馏导致avg@16准确率最高下降17%；退化程度与保留的特权上下文量成正比，在长rollout预算时最严重；特权OPD逆转了普通OPD的增益；特权上下文降低了思维模型rollout的分叉率但不影响指令模型；特权OPD惩罚了普通OPD支持的采样修正token；训练后的思维模型产生更少的验证、回溯和对冲标记。

Conclusion: 针对强思维模型的自我蒸馏需要关注token级信号，特别是在修正和推理步骤周围的信号。特权上下文对指令模型有帮助但会损害更强的思维模型，这一发现揭示了二者在蒸馏行为上的关键差异。

Abstract: Self-distillation is a promising recipe for self-improvement in language models. In this setting, a model can serve as its own teacher when given privileged information, such as a solution to a math problem. This seems especially appealing for thinking models, which can use test-time reasoning to absorb the privileged information. Surprisingly, we show that privileged self-distillation degrades thinking models on long reasoning traces: across five Qwen3 and OLMo thinking models evaluated on AIME24, AIME25, and HMMT25, privileged-context distillation causes a relative drop of up to 17% in avg@16 accuracy. The degradation scales with the amount of privileged context withheld from the student and is most pronounced at long rollout budgets, where thinking models otherwise obtain their largest gains. This failure mode is not specific to self-distillation: on-policy distillation (OPD) improves thinking models, but privileged OPD reverses these gains. Our diagnostics link this failure mode to how privileged teacher context reshapes learning at high-entropy forking positions, where multiple continuations remain plausible and may lead to different reasoning paths. Privileged context lowers fork rates in thinking-model rollouts but not in instruction-model rollouts. This leads to an interesting dichotomy, where privileged context can help instruction-tuned models but hurts stronger thinking models. The effect is visible when the student begins a self-correction branch, where privileged OPD penalizes sampled reconsideration tokens that vanilla OPD supports. Thinking models trained with a privileged teacher produce fewer verification, backtracking, and hedging markers, even after length normalization. These findings indicate that self-distillation for strong thinking models requires attention to token-level signal, especially around correction and reasoning steps.

</details>


### [194] [ClassicLogic: A Knowledge-Driven Benchmark of Classic Puzzle Games for Evaluating Compositional Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05185)
*Mahnoor Shahid,Hannes Rothe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ClassicLogic是一个新基准测试套件，通过四个经典逻辑谜题（数独、KenKen、填字游戏、不等号数独）及其分层显式知识库，评估AI模型在组合泛化和策略组合推理方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组合泛化基准多聚焦于语言任务，缺乏复杂且显式的组合结构，难以系统评估AI在策略层面的组合推理能力。

Method: 构建四个经典逻辑谜题的分层显式知识库，将复杂求解策略形式化定义为对更简单基础策略的组合，支持对推理能力从基本规则学习到多步组合策略应用的细粒度评估。

Result: 提出了一个具有数学验证难度递增的开放源码基准测试，为神经符号系统及其他高级AI推理系统提供了新的挑战性测试平台。

Conclusion: ClassicLogic通过结构化策略组合方式，为评估和推动AI的组合泛化与推理能力提供了有价值的测试环境。

Abstract: Compositional generalization, the ability to understand and produce novel combinations of known components, remains a fundamental challenge for modern artificial intelligence. While few benchmarks exist, many focus on linguistic tasks and lack complex, explicit compositional structures. We introduce ClassicLogic, a new benchmark suite designed to evaluate an agent's ability to learn and compose problem-solving strategies. The benchmark consists of four classic logic puzzles: Sudoku, KenKen, Kakuro, and Futoshiki. Its core innovation is a hierarchical, explicit knowledge base for each game, where complex solving strategies are formally defined as compositions of simpler, foundational strategies. This structure allows for fine-grained evaluation of an agent's reasoning capabilities, from learning basic rules to applying multi-step compositional strategies to solve puzzles of increasing, mathematically validated difficulty. The open-source benchmark provides a challenging new testbed for advancing neuro-symbolic and other advanced AI reasoning systems.

</details>


### [195] [Reason, Reward, Refine: Step-Level Errors Corrections with Structured Feedback for Physics Reasoning in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05199)
*Raj Jaiswal,Dhruv Jain,Rishabh Dhawan,Sree Krishna Uppalapati,Shin'ichi Satoh,Tanuja Ganu,Rajiv Ratn Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种步骤级奖励框架，通过识别推理中的首个错误并生成针对性结构化反馈，结合KL正则化的策略梯度训练模型修正回答，无需暴露真实答案或构建偏好数据。在五个物理基准上，准确率相比CoT提升17-20%，相比最强基线提升10-16%，计算错误率从56.9%降至23.5%，理解错误率从22.3%降至12.0%，但概念错误仍是最难解决的失败模式（89.7%→68.7%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型在物理推理中结构性失败：任何一步的错误都会向前传播，污染后续所有推理。有限的领域知识、多步推导下的幻觉以及分布敏感性进一步加剧了这种失败。

Method: 提出步骤级奖励框架，包含三个核心：1) 识别推理链中首个错误步骤；2) 生成针对该错误的结构化反馈；3) 使用带KL正则化的策略梯度训练模型修正回答，无需暴露真实答案作为生成目标，也无需构建偏好数据，外部验证器仅在训练时使用。

Result: 在五个物理基准上，准确率比CoT提示提升17-20%，比最强基线提升10-16%；计算错误从56.9%降至23.5%，理解错误从22.3%降至12.0%，概念错误从89.7%降至68.7%。

Conclusion: 该方法有效缓解了物理推理中的错误传播问题，显著降低了计算和理解类错误，但概念错误仍是所有条件下最难克服的失败模式。

Abstract: Physics reasoning fails structurally in small language models: an error at any step propagates forward, corrupting every inference that follows. Limited domain knowledge, hallucination under multi-step derivation, and distributional sensitivity compound this failure. We propose a step-level reward framework that identifies the first reasoning error, generates targeted structured feedback, and trains the model to revise its solution via policy gradient with KL regularization, without exposing it to ground truth solutions as generation targets. Unlike annotation-dependent step-level methods, no preference data construction is required and the external verifier operates exclusively at training time. Across five physics benchmarks, our framework delivers accuracy gains of 17-20% over CoT prompting and 10-16% over the strongest baseline, reduces calculation errors from 56.9% to 23.5%, and reduces miscomprehension errors from 22.3% to 12.0% in the best observed cases. Conceptual errors reduce from 89.7% to 68.7%, yet persist as the hardest failure mode across all conditions.

</details>


### [196] [EvoAgentBench: Benchmarking Agent Self-Evolution via Ability Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05202)
*Xingze Gao,Chuanrui Hu,Hongda Chen,Pengfei Yao,Zhao Wang,Yi Bai,Zhengwei Wu,Yunyun Han,Xiaofeng Cong,Jie Gui,Yafeng Deng,Teng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoAgentBench基准，用于评估长程LLM智能体的自我进化能力——即从过往经验中提取可复用过程性知识（搜索、调试、验证等程序），并在四个智能体领域（网页研究、算法推理、软件工程、知识工作）中测试跨任务迁移效果。基准包含528/267训练/测试任务划分，实验表明基于Ability的迁移策略在不同模型间稳定有效，但尚无自动化方法在所有场景中保持正向收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准仅测试单回合任务解决能力，记忆基准仅关注信息保留而非过程性知识的重用，缺乏对智能体从经验中提取可复用程序并实现跨任务迁移的评估体系。

Method: 从智能体执行轨迹中提取Ability（可操作的过程性知识单元），将其规范化后构建领域特定的Ability图谱，通过图谱揭示任务间的过程性重叠关系，确保每个测试任务都有已验证的训练侧Ability支持。

Result: 在528/267任务划分、两种脚手架和三种基础模型上的实验表明，精心策划的Ability内容在不同模型家族间可靠迁移，但当前所有自动化方法均无法在所有设置中维持正向收益。

Conclusion: EvoAgentBench将自我进化评估从聚合准确率比较转向对经验编码、路由和吸收的细粒度诊断，为智能体持续学习提供了新的评估范式。

Abstract: Agent self-evolution in long-horizon LLM systems is largely procedural: useful experience is not merely stored information, but reusable procedures for searching, debugging, and verification. Yet current evaluations do not isolate this form of transfer. Agent benchmarks test single-episode task solving; memory benchmarks target information retention rather than procedural reuse. We introduce EvoAgentBench, a benchmark for agent self-evolution via Ability-guided transfer across four agentic domains: web research, algorithmic reasoning, software engineering, and knowledge work. EvoAgentBench extracts trace-grounded Abilities from agent executions, canonicalizes them into operational units, and builds domain-specific Ability Graphs linking tasks that share procedural overlap. By design, every test task is backed by verified training-side Ability support. Across a 528/267 train/test split, two scaffolds, and three backbones, curated Ability content transfers reliably across model families, but no current automatic method sustains positive gain in all settings. EvoAgentBench shifts self-evolution evaluation from aggregate accuracy comparison to fine-grained diagnosis of experience encoding, routing, and uptake. The benchmark is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/EverMind-AI/EvoAgentBench.

</details>


### [197] [MoP-JEPA: Hard-Assigned Predictor Mixtures for Stochastic JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05238)
*Zhi Song,Ximing Xing,Zhenchao Tang,hanbo Huang,Tianxu Lv,minghao Yang,Zhongzheng Niu,He Bing,Lusheng Wang,Jianhua Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出JEPA世界模型在随机环境中因使用确定性回归预测器会导致结构性问题——预测结果坍缩为条件均值（即不存在于任何实际状态中的中间点）。作者证明硬分配混合预测器（MoP-JEPA）能收敛到转移分布的量化结果（每个预测头对应一个后继模式），并在OGBench离线数据和真实环境中显著超越确定性预测器、门控MoE和变分方法。此外，提出了一套验证协议（含代码本控制、打乱上下文测试、路由门控读等）以消除覆盖漏洞，在验证标准下性能提升2-5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准JEPA使用单一确定性预测器进行潜在回归，在随机环境中无法处理分支转移，预测输出在回归最优下坍缩为后继嵌入的条件均值，对应不存在的状态，导致世界模型无法用于规划。

Method: 提出MoP-JEPA，使用硬分配混合专家预测器，每个专家预测头对应一个转移模式；同时设计验证协议：输入无关代码本控制、打乱上下文测试、路由门控读、转移精度守卫、验证路由准则（模型盲提转移图，仅用真实结果验证）。

Result: 在OGBench离线数据上，单预测器滚动规划成功率仅0.02-0.09，而MoP-JEPA预测模式规划达0.85；在验证标准下，该方法在所有三个迷宫中超越最强软替代方案2-5倍；在真实环境中，在最难迷宫中七组基线中排名第二。

Conclusion: 多模态动态决定了JEPA世界模型能否进行规划；硬分配混合预测器是最小且可验证的修复方案。

Abstract: JEPA world models predict the next latent state with a single deterministic predictor trained by latent regression. We show that this fails structurally when the environment is stochastic: at a branching transition, the regression-optimal predictor outputs the conditional mean of the successor embeddings, a point between the true next states that corresponds to no state at all. We prove this collapse for deterministic and gated mixture-of-experts predictors, and prove that MoP-JEPA's hard-assigned predictors converge instead to a quantizer of the transition distribution: one head per successor mode, enumerable in a single forward pass, which is the interface a planner consumes. On official OGBench offline data with leak-free evaluation, planning over single-predictor rollouts performs poorly ($0.02$--$0.09$ success) while planning over our predicted modes reaches up to $0.85$, ahead of deterministic, gated-MoE, and variational predictors on every task. Because multi-prediction evaluation invites coverage freeloading, a verification protocol is part of the method: an input-agnostic codebook control, a shuffled-context test, router-gated readouts, transition-precision guards, and a verified-route criterion in which the model proposes its transition graph blind and ground truth is used only to check the result. Under this criterion our method outperforms the strongest soft alternative on all three mazes ($2$--$5\times$), and the protocol identifies the remaining gap in that baseline's raw scores as routes through predicted transitions that do not exist. The same model executes in the real environment, placing second of seven against the published OGBench baselines on the hardest maze. Multimodal dynamics decide whether a JEPA world model can plan at all; a mixture of predictors with hard assignment is a minimal and verifiable fix.

</details>


### [198] [MetaSkill-Evolve: Recursive Self-Improvement of LLM Agents via Two-Timescale Meta-Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05297)
*Zefeng Wang,Minxi Yan,Jinhe Bi,Sikuan Yan,Volker Tresp,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaSkill-Evolve，一个双时间尺度的递归技能进化框架，让智能体不仅进化任务技能（做什么），还进化改进技能本身（如何改进），在三个智能体基准测试上显著优于无技能、静态技能和单层进化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自改进智能体虽然能从执行轨迹中重写技能文件，但改进过程本身（即改进程序）是固定不变、非递归的，无法适应任务的多样性，缺乏自我进化的递归性。

Method: 提出MetaSkill-Evolve双时间尺度框架：每个分支包含任务技能s和局部元技能m=(ψ,σ,α,π,ε)，分别参数化分析器、检索器、分配器、提议器和进化器五个代理组件。任务技能在快速循环中进化，元技能在慢速循环中通过相同管道自我应用而进化，无需额外模型或目标函数，所有五个代理共享单个冻结骨干网络。

Result: 在OfficeQA、SealQA、ALFWorld三个基准测试上，相比原始骨干网络，保留测试准确率分别提升+23.54、+16.09和+1.92个百分点，优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: MetaSkill-Evolve通过递归的双时间尺度进化框架成功实现了智能体技能的递归自我改进，证明了元技能与任务技能协同进化的有效性。

Abstract: Recent LLM agents tackle increasingly long-horizon, open-ended tasks, and external skills, reusable procedural knowledge supplied to the agent, further extend this capability. However, a fixed, hand-authored skill is rarely optimal, and cannot adapt to the diversity of tasks an agent encounters. Self-improving agents address this by rewriting their own skill files from execution traces, yielding meaningful gains on challenging benchmarks. Yet such self-evolution remains non-recursive: it improves only the task skill (what the agent does) while the improvement procedure (how it improves) is authored once and held fixed. We introduce MetaSkill-Evolve, a two-timescale framework that makes agentic skill improvement recursive: every branch carries both a task skill $s$ and a branch-local meta-skill $m=(ψ,σ,α,π,\varepsilon)$ whose five components parameterise the Analyzer, Retriever, Allocator, Proposer, and Evolver agents of the improvement pipeline. Task skills evolve on a fast loop while the meta-skill evolves on a slower one under the same pipeline applied to itself, with no additional model or objective. With all five pipeline agents sharing a single frozen backbone, MetaSkill-Evolve outperforms no-skill, static-skill, and single-level evolution baselines on three agentic benchmarks (OfficeQA, SealQA, ALFWorld), improving held-out test accuracy over the raw backbone by +23.54, +16.09, and +1.92 points respectively.

</details>


### [199] [Evaluating and Understanding Model Editing for Medical Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05310)
*Guli Zhu,Chenwei Wu,Liyue Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: M3Bench是一个面向医学视觉语言模型的模型编辑基准测试，包含16,276个问题，覆盖多种解剖结构、模态和专业领域。评估发现现有编辑方法在可靠性、精确性和泛化性上各有缺陷，梯度方法局部性差，记忆方法泛化不足且超参数敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态模型编辑基准仅关注通用任务，未能反映临床领域的真实需求与变异性，缺乏一个专门针对医学VLM模型编辑的评估标准。

Method: 构建了M3Bench基准，包含16,276个问题，覆盖多种解剖结构、模态和专业领域，支持单次和顺序编辑。在6种医学和通用VLM上评估了4种代表性编辑方法。

Result: 无方法在所有标准上表现优异。梯度编辑方法泛化强但局部性差，记忆方法保持局部性但缺乏组合泛化能力且对超参数敏感。失败原因可归因于VLM的潜在空间几何结构及不同编辑方法对其的扰动。

Conclusion: M3Bench为医学多模态模型编辑建立了严格的临床压力测试，为更安全的部署后适应性调整提供了可操作的指导。

Abstract: Model editing promises a fast, targeted way to correct post-deployment mistakes in medical vision-language models (VLMs) without costly retraining. However, existing multimodal model editing benchmarks focus on general-purpose tasks and do not reflect realistic clinical domain requirements and variability. To address this, we introduce M3Bench, a clinically grounded benchmark for multimodal model editing that evaluates whether an edit remains reliable, precise, and generalizable under the challenges of image and text variation, modality and protocol shifts, clinical knowledge composition, and temporal progression. M3Bench contains 16,276 questions spanning diverse anatomy, modalities, and specialties, and supports both single and sequential edits.
  By evaluating 4 representative editors across 6 medical and general VLMs, we find that no method excels across all criteria. Gradient-based editors achieve strong transfer but suffer from catastrophic locality violations, whereas memory-based methods preserve locality but lack compositional generality and exhibit high backbone-dependent hyperparameter sensitivity. We further attribute these failures to the latent space geometry of VLMs and how different editing methods shift its landscape. Overall, M3Bench establishes a rigorous clinical stress test for multimodal model editing and offers actionable guidance for safer post-deployment adaptation. The benchmark is publicly available at https://github.com/BioMed-AI-Lab-U-Michgan/M3Bench .

</details>


### [200] [OptiAgent: End-to-End Optimization Modeling via Multi-Agent Iterative Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05346)
*Adriana Laurindo Monteiro,Nayse Fagundes,Gabriel Mattos Langeloh,Gustavo de Oliveira Kanno,Priscila Louise Aguirre,Thiago Costa Rizuti da Rocha,Victor Leme Beltran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OptiAgent是一个多智能体框架，能够将自然语言描述的运筹学问题自动转化为求解器可读的数学公式和可执行代码，采用多循环验证架构和四种专门反馈机制，在LP、MILP和非线性规划任务的4个基准测试中3项达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决运筹学问题中从自然语言描述到数学建模和代码实现的自动化难题，减少人工建模错误，提高求解效率和可审计性。

Method: 提出OptiAgent多智能体框架，优先进行数学建模步骤，由专用智能体提取决策变量和约束等结构，支持迭代自校正；引入新颖的多循环验证架构，包含四种专门化反馈机制，分别针对误解、结构缺陷、数学不一致性、验证失败和代码错误等不同故障模式。

Result: 在LP、MILP和非线性规划任务的4个基准测试中，3项达到最先进性能（SOTA），在剩余数据集上也保持高度竞争力。

Conclusion: OptiAgent通过模块化设计和透明推理过程，不仅提升了求解准确性，还增强了优化问题求解的可审计性和透明度。

Abstract: We propose OptiAgent, a multi-agent framework that, given a natural language description of an Operations Research problem, is able to output a solver-ready mathematical formulation as well as executable code. Our architecture prioritizes the mathematical modeling step, where dedicated agents extract structures, such as decision variables and constraints, enabling iterative self-correction. We introduce a novel multi-loop validation architecture with four specialized feedback mechanisms, each targeting a distinct failure mode such as misinterpretation, structural defects, mathematical inconsistencies, validation failures, and code errors. Alongside accuracy, our modular design improves the process of solving optimization problems by improving transparency, as each agent exposes its reasoning and feedback, making the full modeling process auditable. Our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance on 3 out of 4 benchmarks across LP, MILP, and Nonlinear Programming tasks, while remaining highly competitive on the remaining dataset.

</details>


### [201] [Graph Sparse Sampling: Breaking the Curse of the Horizon in Continuous MDP Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05359)
*Idan Lev-Yehudi,Vadim Indelman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出图稀疏采样（GSS），一种无分支的在线规划算法，通过共享采样未来轨迹来避免树形搜索中随规划深度指数增长的采样预算问题，适用于连续状态/动作空间，并提供了有限样本性能保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续域中的在线规划面临树形搜索方法（如MCTS）的指数级采样预算增长问题，尤其在连续状态或动作空间中，分支结构导致搜索复杂性急剧上升。

Method: 提出图稀疏采样（GSS），采用无分支的图结构共享采样未来轨迹，利用GPU友好的大批量处理，结合启发式方法聚焦计算资源，并通过平滑备份机制处理全秩或低秩生成模拟器。

Result: 理论证明了GSS在离散或连续动作空间下的有限样本性能保证，其边界对规划时长呈多项式依赖；在连续控制仿真中，GSS在长时域规划上显著优于树形规划器，或达到接近最优的性能。

Conclusion: 无分支的图规划可以作为在线控制中树形搜索的互补设计原则，有效避免指数级采样预算依赖。

Abstract: Planning under uncertainty in continuous domains is essential for autonomous systems, yet computationally demanding. Tree-based search methods such as Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) remain popular, but their branching structure can require sampling budgets that grow exponentially with lookahead depth in the worst case. From a tree perspective, continuous state or action spaces become especially challenging, since the planner must decide where to search in an infinite branching hierarchy. We propose Graph Sparse Sampling (GSS), an online planning algorithm that shares sampled futures across many candidate decisions, rather than sampling separate successors for each candidate action. This branch-free graph exposes large GPU-friendly batches, while using heuristics to focus computation. We prove finite-sample performance guarantees for GSS covering full-rank or low-rank generative simulators via smoothed backups, and discrete or sampled continuous action spaces. Under suitable overlap, regularity, and action-coverage conditions, these bounds have polynomial dependence on the planning horizon, formalizing when shared futures can avoid the exponential horizon dependence of tree-shaped sparse sampling. We demonstrate continuous-control simulations where GSS substantially outperforms tree-based planners on long horizons or achieves near-optimal performance, supporting no-branching graph planning as a complementary design principle for online control.

</details>


### [202] [SovereignPA-Bench: Evaluating User-Owned Personal Agents under Evolving Intent, Platform Mediation, and Consent Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05363)
*Dylan Zongmin Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SovereignPA-Bench基准，用于评估个人智能体在保护用户主权（隐私、同意、证据、负担、防操纵）方面的能力，实验表明全主权脚手架设计优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准评估了工具使用、网页导航、个性化等能力，但很少关注智能体是否维护用户主权——即不盲目追求任务完成，而是在演进意图下尊重隐私、获取同意、基于证据行动、减少用户负担、抵抗平台操纵。

Method: 构建SovereignPA-Bench可执行基准，分离智能体可见的ObservableState与评估者可见的HiddenLabels；设置120个主权压力场景，在4个模型家族和8个策略基线上进行3,840条冻结提示轨迹实验，并进行了240个项目的盲审人工审计。

Result: 全主权脚手架在主权得分上优于直接、仅记忆、仅同意、仅证据、ReAct/工具使用、安全提示和法官守卫基线，同时降低了隐私泄露、同意违反、过度让步和操纵捕获。人工审计显示隐私和同意上高度一致，操纵判断上一致性较低。

Conclusion: 个人智能体评估必须超越任务完成指标，转向代表性、知情同意、证据驱动的行动评估，主权保护是智能体可靠性的关键维度。

Abstract: Personal agents are becoming persistent user-owned intermediaries: they remember preferences, filter platform-mediated information, use tools, and negotiate with services. Existing benchmarks evaluate tool use, web navigation, desktop control, personalization, recommendation, and evolving context, but rarely ask whether an agent preserves user sovereignty: advancing the user's current interests while respecting privacy, consent, evidence, user burden, and resistance to manipulative incentives. We introduce SovereignPA-Bench, an executable benchmark for evaluating user-owned personal agents under evolving intent, platform mediation, privacy boundaries, consent constraints, evidence requirements, and burden tradeoffs. The benchmark separates agent-visible ObservableState from evaluator-only HiddenLabels, reports component metrics for task success, alignment, privacy, consent, evidence, manipulation, burden, and auditability, and preserves paired scenario ordering for model and policy comparisons. We evaluate 120 sovereignty stress scenarios across 4 model families and 8 policy baselines, yielding 3,840 frozen-prompt trajectories with raw prompts, outputs, provider-form responses, parsed actions, recomputable metrics, hard-set analyses, qualitative cases, and a blinded 3-annotator audit over 240 items. Full-sovereign scaffolding improves sovereignty score over direct, memory-only, consent-only, evidence-only, ReAct/tool-use, safety-prompt, and judge-guard baselines while reducing privacy leakage, consent violation, over-concession, and manipulation capture. Human audit shows high agreement on privacy and consent and lower agreement on manipulation, identifying the subjective frontier of platform-persuasion judgments. These results show that personal-agent evaluation must move beyond task completion toward representative, consent-aware, evidence-grounded action.

</details>


### [203] [LLM-as-a-Verifier: A General-Purpose Verification Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05391)
*Jacky Kwok,Shulu Li,Pranav Atreya,Yuejiang Liu,Yixing Jiang,Chelsea Finn,Marco Pavone,Ion Stoica,Azalia Mirhoseini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-as-a-Verifier，一种无需额外训练即可对智能体任务提供细粒度反馈的通用验证框架。通过计算评分token logits分布的期望值生成连续分数，并沿评分粒度、重复评估、标准分解三个维度扩展验证能力。在多个基准上达到SOTA，并可应用于RL提升样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别出"验证"（判断解决方案正确性的能力）作为预训练、后训练和测试时计算之外的新的扩展轴，并验证其有效性。

Method: 提出LLM-as-a-Verifier框架：不直接要求LLM输出离散分数，而是计算评分token logits分布的期望值生成连续分数。通过三个维度扩展验证：(1)评分粒度；(2)重复评估；(3)标准分解。同时提出基于连续分数的成本高效排序算法选择最佳方案。

Result: 在Terminal-Bench V2 (86.5%)、SWE-Bench Verified (78.2%)、RoboRewardBench (87.4%)和MedAgentBench (73.3%)上达到SOTA。细粒度信号可作为任务进度估计代理，并为RL（SAC、GRPO）提供密集反馈提升样本效率。

Conclusion: 验证可作为LLM能力扩展的新轴，连续概率化评分相比离散评分在多个维度上具有可扩展性优势，且生成的细粒度信号可迁移至任务监控和强化学习等下游应用。

Abstract: Scaling pre-training, post-training, and test-time compute have become the central paradigms for improving the capabilities of LLMs. In this work, we identify verification, the ability to determine the correctness of a solution, as a new scaling axis. To unlock this and demonstrate its effectiveness, we introduce LLM-as-a-Verifier, a general-purpose verification framework that provides fine-grained feedback for agentic tasks without requiring additional training. Unlike standard LM judges that prompt LLMs to produce discrete scores for candidate solutions, LLM-as-a-Verifier computes the expectation over the distribution of scoring token logits to generate continuous scores. This probabilistic formulation enables verification to scale along multiple dimensions: (1) score granularity, (2) repeated evaluation, and (3) criteria decomposition. In particular, we show that scaling the scoring granularity leads to better separation between positive and negative solutions, resulting in more calibrated comparisons. Moreover, scaling repeated evaluation and criteria decomposition consistently lead to additional gains in verification accuracy through variance and complexity reduction. We further introduce a cost-efficient ranking algorithm for selecting the best solution among candidates using the verifier's continuous scores. LLM-as-a-Verifier achieves state-of-the-art performance on Terminal-Bench V2 (86.5%), SWE-Bench Verified (78.2%), RoboRewardBench (87.4%), and MedAgentBench (73.3%). Beyond verification, the fine-grained signals from LLM-as-a-Verifier can also serve as a proxy for estimating task progress. We build an extension for Claude Code, enabling developers to monitor and improve their own agentic systems. Finally, we show that LLM-as-a-Verifier can provide dense feedback for RL, improving the sample efficiency of SAC and GRPO on robotics and mathematical reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [204] [When Should Service Agents Reconsider? Difficulty-Routed Control in Customer-Service Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01426)
*Qian Chen,Chengyuan Liu,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种难度路由的服务控制架构，通过轻量级路由器将常规会话保持在低成本基线路径，将存在操作耦合的会话路由到升级工作流，在关键后端写入前集中进行冲突感知沟通和写入触发重新考虑，在零售和航空任务中提升了可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主客服智能体从对话接口转向操作执行角色（如退款、取消、改签等），产生了服务控制问题：既要保持常规服务快速低摩擦，又要防止操作错误。现有方法对全部会话施加统一控制，效率低下。

Method: 提出难度路由的服务控制架构：(1) 轻量级路由器判断会话是否需要升级；(2) 升级路径使用冲突感知沟通和写入触发重新考虑机制，在关键后端写入前集中进行推理和安全检查。

Result: 在零售任务中，该方法在存在操作冲突的请求上持续提升可靠性。路由证据表明更强的控制被导向冲突请求而非常规请求。对话和工具使用特征显示改进来自证据收集、写入分离和写入前重新考虑，而非无差别扩展交互或工具链。航空任务结果验证了方法的通用性。

Conclusion: 难度路由的服务控制架构能有效区分常规和操作耦合的会话，在关键写入前集中安全机制，实现了效率与可靠性的平衡，且具有跨领域泛化能力。

Abstract: Autonomous customer-service agents are shifting from conversational interfaces toward operational execution roles: they retrieve firm records, apply service policies, and execute backend writes such as refunds, cancellations, exchanges, order modifications, and reservation changes. This shift creates a service-control problem: firms must keep routine service fast and low-friction while preventing operational errors on requests where customer instructions, policy constraints, firm records, and backend writes interact. We propose a difficulty-routed service-control architecture that asks when service agents should reconsider before acting. A lightweight router keeps routine sessions on a low-cost baseline path and routes operationally coupled sessions to an escalated workflow. The escalated path uses conflict-aware communication and write-triggered reconsideration to concentrate deliberation and safeguards before consequential backend writes, rather than applying additional control uniformly across all service sessions. We evaluate the architecture on human-verified retail and airline tasks from $τ^{2}$-bench. In retail, the method improves reliability consistently on service requests with operational conflict. Routing evidence shows that stronger control is directed toward conflicted requests rather than broadly applied to routine ones. Dialogue and tool-use profiles suggest that gains do not come from indiscriminate interaction expansion or broader tool chains; instead, added turns and tool calls support evidence gathering, write separation, and pre-write reconsideration. Case-level evidence shows that the escalated workflow preserves fallback plans, binds retrieved records to the correct action, sequences writes, and decomposes multi-entity requests. Airline results extend the same service-control logic to reservation operations.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [The agent creates, we validate: A Lightweight Framework for Agentic Artifact Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02615)
*Yaniv Melamed,Yoni Zukerman,Michal Shechter,Miri Weissler,Ashwin Patil,Hani Neuvirth-Telem*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个轻量级框架，核心原则是“LLM生成，我们验证”，通过测试驱动生成、确定性+LLM测试、专家精炼评判器三个关键属性，将责任从生成质量转移到验证严谨性，并在微软Sentinel安全领域三个工件类型上进行了生产部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM生成结构化工件（如数据库查询、威胁框架映射、实体模式）相对直接，但使其足够可靠以用于生产部署仍面临挑战。

Method: 提出轻量级框架，基于三个关键属性：(1) 测试驱动生成：测试失败时LLM接收提示性错误消息，理解错误并优化后续尝试；(2) 确定性测试与LLM测试：确定性测试捕获可编程验证的启发式规则，LLM测试评估细微语义特征；(3) 专家精炼评判器：LLM测试校准以复制人类专家决策分布，将人工质量门转化为可扩展的评估代理。

Result: 在微软Sentinel生产环境中，对三种安全领域工件（KQL查询生成、MITRE ATT&CK映射、实体映射）进行了部署验证。

Conclusion: 该框架可推广至安全领域之外的其他工件生成任务，为在不牺牲LLM生成效率的情况下获得可靠、高质量输出提供了路径。

Abstract: Generating structured artifacts with Large Language Models - e.g. database queries, threat framework mappings, entity schemas - is relatively straightforward; however, making them reliable enough for production deployments presents challenges. We present a lightweight framework based on a core principle: LLMs generate, we validate. This reframing shifts responsibility from generation quality to validation rigor. The framework rests on three key attributes: First, test driven generation: when tests fail, the LLM receives indicative error messages that expose why the output failed, enabling the LLM to understand its mistakes and refine subsequent attempts. Second, deterministic and LLM-based tests: deterministic tests catch heuristics that can be programmatically verified (schema, syntax, cross-reference), while LLM-based tests evaluate nuanced semantic and delicate features that resist programmatic inspection (intent alignment, logical consistency, domain correctness). Third, expert-distilled judges: LLM-based tests are calibrated to distill and replicate human expert decision distribution, transforming manual human quality gates into scalable, reusable evaluation proxies that reflect professional-grade validation standards. We demonstrate the framework on three artifact types in the security domain - KQL query generation, MITRE ATT\&CK mapping, and entity mapping - deployed in production at Microsoft Sentinel. We believe this framework can be applied beyond security to other artifact generation tasks, providing a path to reliable, high-quality outputs without sacrificing the efficiency gains of LLM generation.

</details>


### [206] [RES-DARE: Failure-Aware Expert Adaptation and Rollback-Safe Self-Repair for Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02687)
*Rahil Aftab,Anyash Prasad,Soumya Mazumdar,Vineet Kumar Rakesh,Tapas Samanta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RES-DARE是一个故障感知的持续入侵检测框架，通过递归专家特化、故障缓冲、HDBSCAN故障区域发现和AEHM-v2回滚安全修复机制，在分布漂移条件下实现稳健的自修复检测。在CICIDS2017、UNSW-NB15和TON_IoT上分别达到0.9850、0.9736和0.9691的macro-F1，且在特征损坏下保持高攻击检测率并实现近零灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统入侵检测系统在静态基准下训练，但实际网络环境存在流量漂移、传感器噪声、工作负载变化和攻击行为演化等分布漂移，导致静态检测器产生自信但错误的预测，造成无声且不安全的故障模式。

Method: 提出RES-DARE框架，核心组件包括：监督对比编码器、两遍专家路由器、故障缓冲机制、基于HDBSCAN的故障区域发现、信任-风险监视器，以及AEHM-v2回滚安全修复机制——候选适应被临时激活，仅在macro-F1保持或提升且信任风险稳定时才提交，否则回滚至最后验证状态。

Result: 在CICIDS2017、UNSW-NB15和TON_IoT上macro-F1分别为0.9850、0.9736和0.9691；高斯特征损坏强度0.10时，CICIDS2017上Attack-F1保留0.7920，灾难性遗忘指标F=0.0015，近零遗忘。

Conclusion: RES-DARE在退化条件下显著提升了鲁棒性、预警能力和部署安全性，验证了故障感知持续学习与回滚安全机制在入侵检测领域的有效性。

Abstract: Intrusion detection systems are often trained under static benchmark conditions, although deployed network environments are affected by traffic drift, sensor noise, changing workloads, and evolving attack behaviour. Under such distribution shifts, static detectors may produce confident but incorrect predictions, leading to silent and unsafe failure modes. In this paper, RES-DARE (Recursive Evolving Specialists-Digital Adaptive Reasoning Engine) is proposed as a failure-aware continual intrusion detection framework with rollback-safe self-repair. Difficult, uncertain, and misclassified samples are treated as failure signals for expert specialisation rather than being discarded as noise. A supervised contrastive encoder, two-pass expert router, failure-buffer mechanism, HDBSCAN-based failure-region discovery, and trust-risk monitor are integrated to support adaptive IDS behaviour. AEHM-v2 is introduced as a rollback-safe repair mechanism, where candidate adaptations are provisionally activated and committed only when macro-F1 is preserved or improved while trust risk remains stable. Otherwise, the system is rolled back to its last validated state. RES-DARE is evaluated on CICIDS2017, UNSW-NB15, and TON\_IoT, achieving macro-F1 scores of 0.9850, 0.9736, and 0.9691, respectively. Under Gaussian feature corruption at strength 0.10, RES-DARE retains an Attack-F1 of 0.7920 on CICIDS2017 and achieves near-zero catastrophic forgetting with F = 0.0015. The results show that RES-DARE improves robustness, warning capability, and deployment safety under degraded conditions.

</details>


### [207] [Not All Refusals Are Equal: How Safety Alignment Fails Cybersecurity at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02714)
*Vadym Hadetskyi,Dario Pasquini,Artem Sorokin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对24个开源LLM的大规模消融实验，发现领域特定的消融（abliteration）是可行的，并以万亿参数规模的Kimi K2为例展示了标准方法。研究发现，拒绝机制在LLM中广泛分布于各层，尤其是在万亿参数MoE架构中。通过捕获网络安全领域中有害概念的部分，实现了领域特定的安全对齐消融。研究还识别出安全训练类型和架构是影响消融效果的最可靠预测因子，并将模型分为3个消融敏感度等级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全对齐虽重要，但它在概念上不区分不同领域和查询的潜在危害程度，这给网络安全等场景带来问题——在这些场景中，模型不应被安全电路约束以完成合法授权任务。因此需要探索领域特定的消融方法，使模型在特定领域（如网络安全）能够解除安全限制而保持其他领域的安全对齐。

Method: 对24个开源LLM进行大规模消融实验，以万亿参数的Kimi K2为例；基于拒绝机制在LLM中占据多维子空间且广泛分布于各层的发现，专门捕获表示网络安全领域有害概念的部分；分析模型特征与消融效果的相关性。

Result: 领域特定的消融是可行的；拒绝机制在LLM中广泛分布于各层，尤其在万亿参数MoE架构中；安全训练类型和架构是消融效果最可靠的预测因子；将模型划分为3个消融敏感度等级。

Conclusion: 领域特定消融（abliteration）在网络安全领域可行且有效，有助于在保持安全对齐整体性的同时，为特定领域（如网络安全）提供必要的灵活性。模型的消融敏感性可通过安全训练类型和架构进行预测。

Abstract: There is no doubt that safety alignment is an essential step in LLM training. However, conceptually it does not distinguish between various domains and the level of potential harm of a query, which creates significant complications in the fields like cyber security, where a model should not be constrained by its safety circuits to accomplish the goals of legitimate, authorized operations. In this work, we share our findings from a large scale abliteration experiment on 24 open-source LLMs and show that domain-specific abliteration is achievable with standard methodology on the example of a 1T-parameter Kimi K2. Building on recent work showing that refusal in LLMs occupies a multi-dimensional subspace within layers, we find that it is also distributed widely across layers, especially in trillion-parameter MoE architectures, and so we aim to capture the part of it that represents harmful concepts in the cybersecurity domain exclusively. We also investigate the correlation between models' features and the effect of domain-specific abliteration, identifying that the type of safety training and architecture are the most reliable predictors. Finally, we classify the models into 3 \emph{abliteration susceptibility} tiers and put forward a set of conjectures as to why a particular effect from this intervention might be observed in a given model.

</details>


### [208] [Vision Token Manipulation Attacks on Cloud-Edge Inference of Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02819)
*Zikai Zhang,Rui Hu,Olivera Kotevska,Jiahao Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了云边端大型视觉语言模型推理过程中视觉令牌传输环节的新攻击面，提出黑盒中间人攻击下的视觉令牌操纵攻击（VTM-Attack），仅操纵10%的令牌即可使模型准确率下降最高88.31%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云边端LVLM推理中，视觉令牌从边缘设备传输到云服务器，这一通信链路暴露了新的攻击面，但目前缺乏对该安全漏洞的系统研究。

Method: 在黑盒中间人攻击场景下，提出四种朴素攻击策略和一种基于优化的令牌选择方法，在预算约束下拦截并操纵部分传输的视觉令牌。

Result: 在6个LVLM（3B-72B）和4个基准测试上，仅操纵10%的视觉令牌即可使准确率下降最高88.31%。

Conclusion: 云边端LVLM推理存在严重安全漏洞，视觉令牌传输环节极易受到中间人攻击。

Abstract: Cloud-edge Large Vision-Language Model (LVLM) inference enables efficient deployment by splitting computation between edge devices and cloud servers. In this process, intermediate vision tokens are transmitted from the edge to the cloud over a communication link, thereby exposing a new attack surface. We study vision token manipulation attack (VTM-Attack) under a black-box man-in-the-middle setting, where an adversary intercepts and manipulates a subset of transmitted vision tokens under a budget constraint. We propose four naïve attack strategies and an optimization-based token selection method. Experiments on 6 state-of-the-art LVLMs (3B-72B) across 4 benchmarks show that manipulating only 10\% of vision tokens can reduce accuracy by up to 88.31\%. These results reveal a critical vulnerability in cloud-edge LVLM inference.

</details>


### [209] [Git Hash Chain Malleability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02820)
*Jacob Ginesin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现Git提交签名存在"哈希链可塑性"漏洞：攻击者无需私钥即可在保持树、元数据和有效签名不变的情况下，构造具有不同提交哈希的第二份提交，并通过Github验证。该漏洞源于ECDSA、OpenPGP子包和S/MIME DER编码的可塑性，影响哈希提交阻塞、依赖锁定和可重现构建系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Git提交签名被广泛认为是不可篡改的证据，保证提交哈希唯一且不可变地标识签名内容。研究者希望验证这一假设的可靠性。

Method: 通过三种数据表示的可塑性路径实现：①ECDSA代数逆元变换(s→n-s)；②OpenPGP未哈希子包结构插入(RFC4880 5.2.3)；③S/MIME CMS信封内非规范DER长度重编码(X.690 10.1)。

Result: ECDSA代数逆元和子包插入可通过本地验证(git verify-commit)，三种方法均能在Github上获得持续的"Verified"认证标记。

Conclusion: Git提交签名不保证哈希唯一性，对依赖链上的哈希阻塞、依赖锁定(Nixpkgs、Go模块、GitHub Actions)和可重现构建系统构成实际威胁。

Abstract: Git commit signing is widely entrusted to serve as evidence that a commit hash uniquely and immutably identifies a specific piece of signed content. We show this invariant does not hold. Given any signed commit, an attacker without access to the signing key, and without breaking SHA2 can produce a second, distinct commit with an identical tree, identical metadata, a valid signature, and a ``Verified'' badge from a Git Forge such as Github, differing only in its commit hash. The modified commit cascades to modify the values of all the subsequent, dependent commit hashes, hence we introduce the terminology ``hash chain malleability'' to describe this phenomenon. The malleability in signed Git hashes is feasible due to the inherent malleability present in many of the data representations that make up a commit. In this paper we show three such malleation routes: (i) algebraic inversion s -> n-s for ECDSA; (ii) structural insertion of an unhashed OpenPGP subpacket (RFC4880 5.2.3) for RSA and EdDSA; and (iii) non-canonical DER length re-encoding (X.690 10.1) inside the CMS envelope for S/MIME. Algebraic inversion for ECDSA signatures and subpacket insertion were found to pass local verification (git verify-commit), and all three methods yield a persistent, independent ``Verified'' record on Github. We discuss the consequences of Git hash chain malleation for hash-based commit blocking, dependency pinning (Nixpkgs, Go modules, Github Actions), and reproducible-build systems that treat the commit hash as a content-addressable primary key, and we provide proof-of-concept tooling that automates all three routes.

</details>


### [210] [JavaVulBench: A Java Vulnerability Benchmark with Realistic Splits, a Unified Multi-Backend Harness, and a Leakage-Aware Evaluation Mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02825)
*Norbert Sandor Szolnoki,Gabor Antal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发布 JavaVulBench，一个包含约30,600个Java方法、涵盖1,740个CVE和700多个项目、具备方法级和行级标注的漏洞检测基准数据集与评估框架，支持五种数据划分策略、三种后端的统一评估接口，并提供预训练污染审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Java漏洞检测基准在数据规模、标注粒度、时间信息、污染审计和统一评估框架方面存在不足，难以公平、全面地评估各类模型在真实场景下的漏洞检测能力。

Method: 构建包含约30,600个Java方法的大规模数据集，标注至方法级和行级，附带CVE发布日期；设计五种数据划分策略（随机、项目不重叠、时间、去重、未见CWE家族）；开发统一LlmPrediction评估接口，支持分类器、本地生成模型和API模型三类后端；为每个模型提供预训练污染审计。

Result: 在统一条件下评估了12个参考检测器，包括CodeBERT、GraphCodeBERT、UniXcoder、DeepSeek-Coder-1.3B以及8个API/开源LLM，所有结果可通过单条命令复现。

Conclusion: JavaVulBench为Java漏洞检测研究提供了标准化、可复现、公平比较的基准平台，污染审计机制有助于区分模型真实泛化能力与记忆效应。

Abstract: We release \textsc{JavaVulBench}, a benchmark dataset and evaluation harness for Java vulnerability detection. The dataset contains $\sim$30{,}600 Java methods spanning 1{,}740 CVEs and 700+ projects, labelled at both method and line granularity, with per-CVE publication dates and five realistic split strategies: random, project-disjoint, temporal, deduplicated, and unseen CWE-family. The harness provides a single \texttt{LlmPrediction} schema across three backend families (encoder classifiers, local generative models served by Ollama, and API-served LLMs routed through OpenRouter) so that twelve reference detectors CodeBERT, GraphCodeBERT, UniXcoder, DeepSeek-Coder-1.3B, and eight API/open-weight LLMs (GPT-4o, GPT-4.1-mini, Claude Sonnet~4, DeepSeek-v3, DeepSeek-Coder-v2, Qwen-2.5-Coder-14B/7B, CodeLlama-13B) are evaluated under identical conditions from a single command. A pre-training contamination audit is shipped alongside every model so users can separate genuinely unseen test CVEs from potentially memorised ones. Data, code, and fine-tuned checkpoints are archived on Zenodo [31] and short demonstration video is available on YouTube (https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nMTX\_hqkuoM) https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nMTX_hqkuoM.

</details>


### [211] [ShannonProver: Towards Automating Formal Cryptographic Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02847)
*Yiping Ma,Yu-Lin Tsai,Mayank Rathee,Deevashwer Rathee,François Dupressoir,Pierre-Yves Strub,Raluca Ada Popa*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 ShannonProver，一个用于自动化密码学证明的智能体框架。该系统在密码学家提供安全模型和定理分解（将目标定理分解为引理级证明义务）的前提下，自动为这些义务构建 EasyCrypt 证明脚本。在包含教科书原语、部署协议和 NIST 标准化提案等数据集上的评估表明，ShannonProver 能够自动化 ChaChaPoly1305 和 MEE-CBC 等案例中相当大一部分的密码学证明工程工作，从而加速密码学研究的迭代速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码学证明的生成规模日益超出社区手动验证的能力。机器检验的证明提供了可扩展的验证路径，但在 EasyCrypt 等表达力强的证明助手中编写证明脚本仍是主要瓶颈：即使已知高层证明计划，将其转换为证明策略仍需大量推理工作。

Method: ShannonProver 是一个智能体框架，采用"密码学家提供安全模型和定理分解（目标定理到引理级证明义务）→ 系统自动构造 EasyCrypt 证明脚本"的模式，实现密码学证明的自动化。

Result: 在包含教科书原语、部署协议、NIST 标准化提案以及此前未公开的专家案例研究的数据集上进行了评估。结果表明，ShannonProver 能够自动化 ChaChaPoly1305 和 MEE-CBC 等案例中相当大一部分的密码学证明工程工作。

Conclusion: ShannonProver 为加速密码学研究指明了一条路径：当智能体自动化了证明工程负担后，密码学家可以更快地迭代新构造，更早获得机器检验的保证，从而将可信协议从设计更快地推向部署。

Abstract: Cryptographic proofs are produced at a scale that increasingly exceeds the community's ability to verify them manually. Machine-checked proofs offer a path toward scalable proof verification, but writing proof scripts for expressive proof assistants such as EasyCrypt remains a major bottleneck: even when the high-level proof plan is known, converting it into proof tactics requires substantial reasoning effort. This paper presents ShannonProver, an agentic framework for automating cryptographic proofs. ShannonProver targets the setting in which a cryptographer provides the security model and a decomposition of the target theorem into lemma-level proof obligations, while the system automatically constructs EasyCrypt proof scripts for those obligations.
  We evaluate ShannonProver on a dataset of formal cryptographic proofs in EasyCrypt. The dataset spans textbook primitives, deployed protocols, and standardization efforts such as NIST proposals, and includes expert case studies drawn from a corpus that has not previously been available online. We show that ShannonProver can automate substantial portions of cryptographic proof engineering for case studies such as ChaChaPoly1305 and MEE-CBC. More broadly, this work suggests a path toward accelerating cryptographic research: as agents automate the proof-engineering burden, cryptographers can iterate more quickly on new constructions, obtain machine-checked assurance earlier, and bring trustworthy protocols from design to deployment faster.

</details>


### [212] [MOSAIC: Knowledge-Guided CLI Command Composition Attack in LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02857)
*Jiangrong Wu,Huaijin Wang,Yihao Zhang,Yuhong Nan,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CCR（CLI命令组合风险）的新型攻击面，利用LLM编码代理在Unix设计下通过操作系统状态共享的良性命令组合形成危险的生产者-消费者状态关系。作者开发了MOSAIC框架，通过从CVE、安全公告和PoC中提取已验证的命令状态行为，组合成利用路径并实例化为真实开发工作流。在5个真实CLI编码代理和5个后端LLM的2525次试验中，MOSAIC在良性开发任务下实现了96.59%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有攻击和防御主要针对指令层（恶意文本），忽略了通过操作系统状态共享的良性命令组合可能形成的危险生产者-消费者状态关系这一漏洞表面。

Method: 提出MOSAIC框架，采用知识引导方法：从CVE、安全公告和研究者PoC中提取已验证的命令状态行为并总结为可复用摘要，组合这些摘要形成利用路径，再实例化为真实的开发者工作流，用于黑盒代理评估。

Result: 在5个真实CLI编码代理和5个后端LLM上的2525次试验中，MOSAIC在良性开发任务下实现了96.59%的攻击成功率。

Conclusion: CLI命令组合风险（CCR）是LLM编码代理中一个严重且被忽视的攻击面，MOSAIC能够系统性地发现和利用这类漏洞，表明现有安全防御需要扩展以覆盖命令组合层的风险。

Abstract: LLM coding agents increasingly complete development tasks by issuing ordinary CLI commands. Following the Unix design, these commands cooperate through shared operating-system state: one command may write state that a later command reads. While this composition is benign and intended, it creates an overlooked exploit surface. Existing attacks and defenses mainly target the instruction layer, where malicious intent appears as hostile text. In contrast, we observe that individually benign commands can form a dangerous producer-consumer state relation across the command trace, exposing what we call CLI command-composition risk (CCR).
  Given this new attack surface, it is critical to systematically uncover and characterize the impact of CCR in real-world coding agents. However, systematically understanding this risk is quite challenging, because naive command enumeration and end-to-end LLM generation produce mostly invalid workflows. We present MOSAIC, a knowledge-guided framework that distills validated command-state behaviors from CVEs, advisories, and researcher PoCs into reusable summaries, composes them into exploit paths, and instantiates them as realistic developer workflows for black-box agent evaluation. Across five real-world CLI coding agents and five backend LLMs over 2,525 trials, MOSAIC achieves a 96.59% attack success rate under benign developer tasks.

</details>


### [213] [Overprivilege Analysis of Security Policies in Serverless Cloud Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02875)
*Elvis Yeboah-Duako,Pubali Datta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对689个AWS Lambda应用（1,293个函数）的大规模测量研究，发现无服务器计算中存在系统性的权限过度授予问题：47.7%的应用存在多余权限，平均可减少99.65%的权限；通配符定义的权限过度授予比高出274倍，18.8%的应用存在不必要的权限提升能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无服务器计算的面向服务编程模型使得安全策略规范复杂化，现有IAM权限管理已存在普遍配置错误问题，而无服务器应用中函数、服务和资源的多样性以及云提供商频繁的权限模型变更，极大增加了策略错误配置的可能性，导致权限过度授予、攻击面扩大。

Method: 开发了PrivLess静态策略分析框架：从应用源代码提取函数到资源的交互关系，推导交互-权限映射，并将推断出的交互与声明的策略进行比对，以量化权限过度授予程度。

Result: 47.7%的应用存在多余权限，平均权限减少潜力达99.65%；通配符定义权限的应用过度授予比高出274倍；18.8%的应用持有不必要的权限提升能力，12个应用存在不需要的防御规避权限。

Conclusion: 无服务器生态系统中的权限过度授予是系统性的且严重，多余权限能够被利用为具体的攻击向量（权限提升、防御规避），亟需改进策略管理机制。

Abstract: Serverless computing has seen rapid adoption in cloud deployments, yet the security implications of its service-oriented programming model remain poorly understood. Distributed, modular, and heterogeneous applications complicate the specification of precise security policies. Role-based access control solutions such as Identity and Access Management (IAM) already exhibit pervasive misconfiguration problems, and the multiplicity of functions, services, and resources in serverless applications, together with frequent permission model changes by cloud providers, greatly increases the likelihood of policy misconfigurations. Consequently, policies are often overprivileged, thereby enlarging the attack surface and exposing sensitive cloud resources to compromise.
  We present a large-scale measurement study of overprivilege in real-world serverless applications, analyzing a curated dataset of 689 AWS Lambda applications comprised of 1,293 functions. To enable this study, we develop PrivLess, a static policy analysis framework that extracts function-to-resource interactions from application source code, derives an interaction-permission mapping, and reconciles inferred interactions with declared policies to quantify overprivilege. Our measurement reveals that overprivilege is systemic and severe across the serverless ecosystem: 47.7% of applications carry excess permissions with a significant privilege reduction potential of 99.65%. Applications with wildcard-defined permissions exhibited an average overprivilege ratio 274x higher than those without. More critically, the excess permissions enable concrete attack vectors: 18.8% of applications hold unnecessary Privilege Escalation capabilities, and 12 applications had Defense Evasion permissions they did not need.

</details>


### [214] [PPE-Bench: A Benchmark for Evaluating MLLM Unlearning under Private-Public Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02897)
*Xianren Zhang,Delvin Ce Zhang,Dongwon Lee,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对多模态大模型(MLLM)中机器遗忘任务，现有基准存在两大局限：简化图像(仅含单一目标个体)和忽视私有与公共信息视觉纠缠。本文提出PPE-Bench基准，每张图像同时包含需遗忘的私有目标个体和需保留的公共信息(公众人物/地标)，并引入两种简单有效的方法以在遗忘过程中保护公共信息。实验发现现有遗忘方法虽能减少隐私泄露，但常严重损害相邻公共信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM遗忘基准存在两个主要局限：1）使用仅包含单一目标个体的简化图像，无法反映真实照片的视觉复杂性；2）假设遗忘集和保留集完全分离，忽略了私有信息常与良性公共信息在视觉上纠缠的现实(如私人个体与公众人物或知名地标同框)，遗忘私有目标不应破坏公共上下文。

Method: 提出PPE-Bench基准，构建每张图像同时包含需要遗忘的私有目标个体和需要保留的公共信息(公众人物和地标)的数据集。同时引入两种简单有效的辅助方法来保护遗忘过程中的公共信息。

Result: 实验发现现有遗忘方法能够减少私有信息泄露，但往往会在很大程度上损害相邻的公共信息保留能力。

Conclusion: PPE-Bench揭示了现有MLLM遗忘方法在处理私有-公共信息纠缠场景时的不足，为构建更符合现实复杂性的遗忘评估提供了新基准。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown strong capabilities, but they may memorize private information from web data, raising privacy concerns. Machine unlearning offers a way to remove such private knowledge without retraining from scratch. However, existing MLLM unlearning benchmarks have two major limitations. First, they rely on simplified images that contain only the single target individual, failing to reflect the visual complexity of real-world photos. Second, they typically assume that the forget set and retain set are fully separated, ignoring the fact that private information is often visually entangled with benign public information. For example, a private individual may appear with a public figure or in front of a well-known landmark, where unlearning the private target should not damage the public context. To address these limitations, we propose PPE-Bench, a new benchmark for evaluating MLLM unlearning under private-public entanglement. Each image contains a target individual to be forgotten and public information to be preserved, including public figure and landmark. We further introduce two simple but effective methods to better preserve public information during unlearning. Through experiments, we find that existing unlearning methods can reduce private information leakage, but often substantially harm adjacent public information.

</details>


### [215] [TIER: Trajectory-Invariant Explanation Regularization for Membership Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02903)
*Varun Sharma,Kar Wai Fok,Vrizlynn L. L. Thing*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出轨迹不变解释正则化（TIER）方法，利用模型自身梯度作为防御信号，通过对基于梯度引导扰动模拟的置信度下降轨迹施加KL散度正则化和方差缩减，有效缓解基于解释的成员推理攻击，同时保持模型效用和解释保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成员推理攻击可利用解释界面的置信度下降轨迹作为判别特征，而现有防御方法无法有效缓解这种基于解释的攻击。

Method: 提出轨迹不变解释正则化（TIER）方法，通过梯度引导扰动模拟置信度下降轨迹，使用KL散度最小化成员与非成员之间的分布偏移，同时降低两者置信度下降的方差，实现解释层面的自一致性。

Result: 大量实验证明该方法能有效缓解基于解释的成员推理攻击，在提供隐私保护的同时保持模型效用和解释保真度。

Conclusion: 利用模型自身梯度作为防御信号，通过正则化对齐成员与非成员的解释轮廓，是一种有效的隐私防御策略。

Abstract: Explainability is central to building trustworthy AI, yet explanation interfaces can inadvertently provide adversaries with an expanded privacy-related attack surfaces. Recent studies show that advanced membership-inference attacks succeed by exploiting confidence-drop trajectories, induced through attribution-guided perturbations, as discriminative features, rather than directly using confidence scores or explanation vectors. Existing defenses against membership inference fail to directly mitigate such explanation-driven attacks. In this work, we investigate whether, during training, a model's own gradients can be leveraged as defense signals against such attacks, thereby aligning explanation profiles between members and non-members. To this end, we propose a Trajectory-Invariant Explanation Regularization (TIER) defense that penalizes erratic fluctuations in confidence drops simulated through gradient-guided perturbations and simultaneously minimizes the distributional shifts via KL-divergence. Unlike conventional adversarial training, which emphasizes label robustness, our approach targets explanation robustness by enforcing self-consistency through KL-divergence and reducing the variance of confidence drops between members and non-members. Extensive experiments confirm that our method effectively mitigates these attacks, delivering privacy protection while maintaining model utility and explanation fidelity.

</details>


### [216] [PromptPET: Privacy-Utility Optimized Prompt Obfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02932)
*Ke Yang,Olivia Figueira,Umar Iqbal,Athina Markopoulou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用户端机制PROMPTPET，通过四种混淆操作（编辑、抽象、替换和加噪/去噪）来保护用户与AI聊天机器人交互时的隐私。该机制利用数据类型分类法识别和混淆敏感信息，并通过强化学习优化的规则选择器为每个敏感部分选择最佳混淆操作，在隐私保护与实用性之间取得最优平衡，实验表明其优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户在与AI聊天机器人交互时可能有意或无意地泄露敏感信息，这些信息可能被用于用户画像，造成隐私风险。现有方法缺乏对隐私-效用权衡的系统性优化，因此需要一种能够在保护隐私的同时保留足够信息以获取有用响应的用户端机制。

Method: 本文对比了四种混淆操作：编辑（redaction）、抽象（abstraction）、替换（replacement）以及一种新颖的加噪/去噪方案（noising/denoising）。利用数据类型分类法识别和混淆敏感信息，并显式考虑聊天响应的实用性来做出混淆决策。首先独立优化和评估每种混淆操作的隐私-效用权衡；然后提出PROMPTPET，一个基于LLM的智能体，使用强化学习启发的规则优化器为提示中的每个敏感部分选择最佳混淆操作。

Result: 在真实世界聊天数据集上的实验表明，PROMPTPET能够匹配任何单一混淆操作所能达到的最佳隐私-效用权衡，并且显著优于先前的先进方法。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过动态组合多种混淆操作可以更好地平衡隐私保护与任务实用性，且强化学习优化的规则选择器在该场景下具有优越性，为AI聊天中的用户隐私保护提供了有效的新方案。

Abstract: Privacy is an important challenge when users interact with AI chatbots, since users may share sensitive information, explicitly or implicitly, and AI chatbots can use this information for user profiling. In this paper, we aim to protect user privacy via a user-side mechanism that transforms sensitive information in a user prompt, while preserving enough information to elicit a useful response from the chatbot. This approach faces an inherent tradeoff between protecting privacy (i.e., avoiding profiling) and preserving utility (i.e., getting personalized and task-specific responses). To that end, we consider, evaluate, and compare four different obfuscation actions, namely redaction, abstraction, replacement, and a novel noising/denoising scheme that we introduce. Additional novel insights include: utilizing a data type taxonomy to both identify and obfuscate sensitive information and explicitly taking into account the utility of chat responses in making the obfuscation decision. First, we systematically optimize and evaluate each obfuscation action independently in terms of the privacy-utility tradeoff it achieves. Second, we propose PROMPTPET, an LLM-based agent that selects the best obfuscation action for each sensitive part of the prompt, using a reinforcement-learning inspired rule optimizer, applied for the first time in this context. Using a real-world chat dataset, we show that PROMPTPET matches the best privacy-utility tradeoff attainable by any single obfuscation action and significantly outperforms prior state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [217] [Enhanced Feature Extraction for IoT Network Intrusion Detection Using GNNs and KAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02981)
*Long Zhao,Shixun Ji,Bin Cheng,Bin He*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出SKGFusionKAN，一种结合多尺度选择性核注意力机制、边导向消息传递、门控融合和KAN分类器的GNN方法，用于解决IoT网络入侵检测中拓扑动态、流量不平衡和攻击模式复杂的问题，在四个NIDS基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT网络具有动态拓扑、流量不平衡、攻击模式复杂以及极端异构性和稀疏拓扑等特点，传统基于GNN的入侵检测方法难以有效建模节点和边特征，也无法捕获细粒度异常。

Method: 在GraphSAGE基础上引入多尺度选择性核注意力机制实现自适应特征提取；采用边导向消息传递增强信息传播；通过选择性核注意力对不同尺度的边特征进行自适应加权；引入门控融合动态整合多尺度特征以增强对抗演化攻击的鲁棒性；最终利用KAN分类器提供更强的非线性建模能力以检测低频攻击。

Result: 在四个NIDS基准数据集上的二分类和多分类任务中，SKGFusionKAN一致性地超越了当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: SKGFusionKAN作为GNN与KAN的综合性集成方案，在IoT入侵检测领域具有显著潜力，为异构动态网络环境下的安全检测提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advancements in the Internet of Things (IoT) emphasize the urgent need for advanced network security, as IoT networks feature dynamic topologies, imbalanced traffic, and complex attack patterns. Unlike general IT networks, IoT environments exhibit extreme heterogeneity and sparse topologies. Traditional GNN-based intrusion detection methods often struggle to efficiently model node and edge features or capture fine-grained anomalies in such settings. To address this, we propose SKGFusionKAN, a novel IoT-tailored approach enhancing GraphSAGE with a multi-scale selective kernel attention mechanism. This enables adaptive extraction of node and edge features under diverse traffic conditions. Specifically, our edge-oriented message passing strengthens information propagation, while selective kernel attention adaptively weights edge-derived information from different scales to handle heterogeneity. We also introduce a gated fusion process to dynamically integrate multi-scale features, improving robustness against evolving attacks. Finally, we leverage Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KAN) for classification, offering superior nonlinear modeling capabilities essential for detecting intricate, low-frequency attacks.
