To our knowledge, this work presents a comprehensive integration of GNNs and KAN with dedicated architectural innovations for IoT intrusion detection. Extensive experiments on four NIDS benchmarks show that SKGFusionKAN consistently outperforms state-of-the-art approaches in binary and multiclass tasks, demonstrating its potential for IoT security.

</details>


### [218] [Builder, Defender, Breaker: The Case Against Removing the Human from the AI-Driven Security Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03215)
*Mohamed Chahine Ghanem*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文论证了当AI系统同时承担代码编写、安全加固和漏洞探测三种角色时，会导致验证独立性丧失、人类判断被移除、责任主体模糊等根本性问题，主张人类应永久性地存在于安全环路中而非仅作为过渡性脚手架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前AI安全领域中"完全自动化是部分辅助的自然终点"的主流乐观倾向，指出当同一生成模型家族同时承担构建、防御和测试三种角色时，会产生系统性盲区共享、验证独立性丧失等深层问题，需要重新审视人机分工的基本假设。

Method: 综合运用来自自主代码生成、对抗性机器学习、软件容错以及首次全机器黑客竞赛等多个领域的证据，进行跨领域的理论论证和案例分析。

Result: 论证结果显示，将人类从安全环路中移除不仅提升自动化水平，还会造成多重后果：(1) 移除评判机器输出的外部预言机；(2) 超过人类可干预的临界点；(3) 为攻击者提供可预测且可投毒的目标；(4) 消解故障时的责任主体。

Conclusion: 人类应作为永久性结构要求而非临时性脚手架存在于安全环路中，并提出了人机之间可防御的劳动分工应保留的原则。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence has spread across the whole of the security lifecycle. The same family of models now writes application code, hardens it, and probes it for weaknesses, so that a single generative substrate increasingly performs all three roles at once. Enthusiasm for this convergence tends to treat full autonomy as the natural end point of partial assistance. This article argues that it is not. When the system that builds an artifact is drawn from the same distribution as the systems that defend and test it, the three roles inherit a common set of blind spots, and the independence that makes verification meaningful is quietly lost. Removing the human does more than raise the automation level: it collapses the external oracle against which machine output is judged, outruns the point at which a person could intervene, hands adversaries a predictable and poisonable target, and dissolves the locus of accountability when something fails. Drawing on evidence from autonomous code generation, adversarial machine learning, software fault tolerance, and the first all-machine hacking tournaments, we argue that the human belongs in the loop not as a temporary scaffold but as a permanent structural requirement, and set out what a defensible division of labour between people and machines should preserve.

</details>


### [219] [CONTRA: Red-Teaming Configurations of Personalizable Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03220)
*Jonathan Nöther,Adish Singla,Goran Radanovic*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CONTRA算法，一种基于LLM辅助树搜索的红队测试方法，用于发现LLM代理配置中导致恶意行为执行的安全漏洞。研究发现75.1%的公共技能库中存在至少一种可导致恶意行为执行的配置，且大多数未被现有扫描检测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM代理从对话系统发展为完全自主代理（如OpenClaw），其通过可修改内部文件和安装技能实现个性化，但更大的能力和自主性增加了无意中执行恶意行为的风险。现有安全机制对个性化配置带来的风险评估不足。

Method: 提出CONfiguration Tree-search for Red-teaming Agents (CONTRA)，一种LLM辅助的树搜索算法，通过推理良性但危险的配置并在模拟环境中评估，自动发现导致恶意行为执行的代理配置。构建了包含473个公共技能及每个技能2-5个对应恶意目标行为的数据集。

Result: 75.1%的技能存在至少一种可导致恶意行为执行的配置，大多数未被现有扫描检测到。CONTRA在所有测试案例中成功识别出导致目标行为执行的配置的比例为39.2%。

Conclusion: 当前基于LLM的代理在个性化方面提供的安全性不足，亟需更有效的安全机制来防范配置层面的恶意行为风险。

Abstract: Recent tools such as OpenClaw have extended the capabilities of LLM-based agents from simple dialog-based systems to fully autonomous agents. These systems allow personalization of the agent through modifiable internal files and the installation of skills. While this enables deployment in a wide range of settings and the automation of diverse tasks, greater capability and autonomy increases the risk of malicious actions being executed unintentionally. In this work, we explore the interplay between agent configuration and the risk of executing dangerous actions without explicit instruction. To this end, we propose CONfiguration Tree-search for Red-teaming Agents (CONTRA), an LLM-assisted tree-search algorithm that discovers agent configurations resulting in the execution of malicious actions. CONTRA works by reasoning about benign yet dangerous configurations and evaluating them in a simulated environment. We construct a dataset of the 473 most popular skills from a public repository, along with 2-5 corresponding malicious target actions per skill. In a large-scale analysis, we find that 75.1% of skills have at least one configuration resulting in the execution of a malicious action, most of which have not been detected as containing malicious content by existing scans. Overall, CONTRA successfully identifies a configuration leading to the execution of the target action in 39.2% of all tested cases. Our findings demonstrate that current agents provide insufficient safety with respect to personalization.

</details>


### [220] [Agentic and Generative AI for Open-Source Intelligence and Cyber Investigations: Taxonomy, Evaluation, Challenges, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03233)
*Eduardo Almeida Palmieri,Mohamed Chahine Ghanem,Dipo Dunsin,Zubair Baig,Ed de Quincey,Kim-Kwang Raymond Choo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了74项研究，将智能体AI确立为独立于LLM提示工程的类别，识别出“幻觉-验证缺口”这一关键发现，指出了OSINT生命周期中验证、报告等环节的研究不足，并提出了包含十点研究议程的未来方向，最终主张人机协同模式（人类-AI副驾驶）作为近期最可行的部署架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开数字信息快速增长使得人工开源情报分析已不足以满足现代情报、网络安全和网络调查的需求。大语言模型和智能体AI系统虽展现出潜力，但评估框架未能跟上其能力发展步伐。

Method: 对74篇研究进行系统综述，构建包含11个类目的分类法（涵盖LLM基础、智能体架构、RAG、知识图谱、提示工程、领域适应、评估基准与风险等），并就OSINT生命周期映射现有研究。

Result: 识别出幻觉-验证缺口：20余项研究承认幻觉是主要可靠性问题，但仅在1项OSINT专用RAG系统中对端到端幻觉进行了经验测量；现有研究对OSINT生命周期的收集和分析阶段支持较好，但验证、报告、传播和决策支持环节覆盖不足。

Conclusion: 人机协同模式（人类-AI副驾驶），即LLM辅助收集与分类而分析师保留验证与决策责任，是近期最合理的部署架构。

Abstract: The rapid growth of publicly available digital information has rendered manual open-source intelligence (OSINT) analysis insufficient for modern intelligence, cybersecurity, and cyber investigation. Large language models (LLMs) and agentic AI systems, capable of tool use, multi-step reasoning, and iterative intelligence generation, have emerged as promising solutions, yet evaluation frameworks have not kept pace with reported capabilities. This survey systematically reviews 74 studies and makes four contributions. First, it establishes agentic AI as a distinct analytical category rather than an extension of LLM prompting, organising the literature through an 11-category taxonomy covering LLM foundations, agentic architectures, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), knowledge graphs, prompt engineering, domain adaptation, evaluation benchmarks, and risk. Second, it identifies the hallucination-validation gap as a corpus-level finding: although hallucination is recognised as a major reliability concern in over twenty studies, end-to-end hallucination is empirically measured in only one OSINT-specific RAG-based system, non-reproducible conditions, while related reasoning and factual-correction studies evaluate general-domain question answering rather than OSINT. Third, it maps existing research to the OSINT lifecycle, showing strong support for collection and analysis but limited coverage of verification, reporting, dissemination, and decision support. Fourth, it derives a ten-point research agenda addressing evaluation, benchmarking, hallucination measurement, adversarial robustness, dark-web coverage, multimodal intelligence, and governance. It concludes that a human-AI co-pilot model, where LLMs assist collection and triage while analysts retain responsibility for verification and decision-making, represents the most defensible near-term deployment architecture.

</details>


### [221] [Security Analysis for SCONE Logic Locking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03288)
*Akashdeep Saha,Mohammed Nabeel,Johann Knechtel,Michail Maniatakos,Ozgur Sinanoglu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示SCONE逻辑锁定方案的两个变体均存在根本性安全漏洞：无编码状态变体因输入空间维度坍缩而无法提供额外暴力破解维度，有编码状态变体则存在多项式时间白盒攻击可完全恢复线性编码关系。实验验证了所有基准测试上的100%恢复率，并提出了非线性缓解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SCONE方案声称通过扩展逻辑锁定接口、增加额外编码输入来提升安全性，本文旨在验证其真实安全强度，揭示其数学基础和实现层面的根本性缺陷。

Method: 1) 对无编码状态变体进行维度分析，证明线性编码下有效输入空间仍是n维而非n+m维；2) 对有编码状态变体提出多项式时间白盒攻击，从锁定网表中精确恢复附加输入数量和线性编码关系；3) 提出黑盒认证程序，仅从有效编码输入样本即可确认维度坍缩；4) 在ISCAS-85、ITC-99和ARM Cortex-M0 RTL基准上实验验证。

Result: 在所有评估实例上实现100%的编码关系恢复率；在ARM Cortex-M0 RTL基准上同样实现精确白盒恢复；验证了维度坍缩理论；提出的非线性缓解方案能抵御SCONE原论文中所有代表性攻击集。

Conclusion: SCONE方案的线性编码机制存在本质安全缺陷，无编码状态变体的接口扩展是无效的，有编码状态变体可被完全攻破。非线性缓解虽能抵御当前攻击，但仍需进一步安全评估。

Abstract: SCONE [DAC'25] expands a logic locking interface with additional encoded inputs derived from the original primary inputs, and admits two realizations: a \textit{with-ES} variant, where the critical encoding stage is implemented in hardware, and a \textit{without-ES} variant, where the locked design directly exposes an encoded interface of width $n+m$. We show that both realizations are vulnerable, but for different reasons. For the without-ES variant, we prove that, when the added encoded inputs are deterministic linear functions of the original inputs, the valid encoded-input space remains $n$-dimensional despite the nominal expansion to $n+m$ inputs. Hence, the widened interface does not yield $m$ additional or independent brute-force dimensions. For the with-ES variant, we present a polynomial-time white-box attack that exactly recovers the added-input count and the implemented linear encoding relation from the locked netlist, achieving 100\% recovery over all evaluated instances. We also develop a black-box procedure that certifies the same dimensionality collapse from valid encoded-input samples without reconstructing the hidden encoder. Experiments on ISCAS-85 and ITC-99 benchmarks validate both results, and we further demonstrate exact white-box recovery on an ARM Cortex-M0 RTL benchmark. Finally, we propose a lightweight non-linear mitigation and show that it does not exhibit the vulnerabilities identified in this paper under all representative attack sets considered in SCONE.

</details>


### [222] [LLM-Enhanced Hierarchical Heterogeneous Graph Representation Learning for Malicious Python Package Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03350)
*Hang Gao,Xiaoyu Chen,Baoquan Cui,Zhen Tang,Peng Qiao,Fengge Wu,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种LLM增强的层次异构图表示学习框架，用于恶意Python包检测。该框架构建层次异构代码图，利用LLM推断函数级语义角色，并设计层次异构图神经网络进行类型感知的消息传递，实现恶意行为传播建模与包级分类。同时结合函数级归因机制与LLM推理，自动定位细粒度恶意行为。实验表明该方法在真实数据集上优于传统机器学习、图检测器和先进LLM方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的检测方法难以捕捉程序实体间的层次组织和异构交互；LLM虽具强大代码理解能力，但很少与结构化程序表示结合进行细粒度恶意行为分析。

Method: (1) 构建层次异构代码图，显式建模异构代码实体及不同类型的结构依赖关系；(2) 利用LLM推断函数级语义角色，引入语义异构性；(3) 设计层次异构图神经网络，进行类型感知的消息传递；(4) 引入函数级归因机制，结合LLM推理自动定位恶意行为。

Result: 在真实数据集上，该框架在不同规模和依赖复杂度的包上均一致优于传统机器学习方法、图检测器和最先进的LLM，同时提供了准确、鲁棒且可解释的恶意行为定位。

Conclusion: LLM增强的层次异构图表示学习能有效捕获Python包中代码实体的层次结构与异构交互，显著提升恶意包检测精度和可解释性，无需人工专家介入即可实现细粒度恶意行为定位。

Abstract: Malicious Python packages have become a major threat to software supply chain ecosystems due to the widespread adoption of open-source repositories such as PyPI. Existing learning-based detection methods struggle to capture the hierarchical organization and heterogeneous interactions among different program entities. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in code understanding and semantic reasoning, they are rarely integrated with structural program representations for fine-grained malicious behavior analysis. In this paper, we propose an LLM-enhanced hierarchical heterogeneous graph representation learning framework for malicious Python package detection. The framework constructs a hierarchical heterogeneous code graph that explicitly models heterogeneous code entities and different types of structural dependencies. LLMs are further leveraged to infer function-level semantic roles, introducing an additional layer of semantic heterogeneity. Based on this graph, we develop a hierarchical heterogeneous graph neural network that performs type-aware message passing over different node and edge categories, effectively modeling malicious behavior propagation for accurate package-level classification. The framework also incorporates a function-level attribution mechanism which, combined with LLM reasoning, automatically identifies suspicious functions and localizes fine-grained malicious behaviors without human expert intervention. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets show that our framework consistently outperforms traditional machine learning methods, graph-based detectors, and state-of-the-art LLMs across packages with varying sizes and dependency complexities, while providing accurate, robust, and interpretable malicious behavior localization.

</details>


### [223] [Scalable Differentially Private Data Compression via Diffusion and Stochastic Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03392)
*Gergely Flamich,Oykü Sıla Güner,Yanxiao Liu,Deniz Gündüz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DP-DiPP，一种结合随机编码与扩散模型的差分隐私图像压缩流水线，通过扩展泊松私有表示（PPR）编码隐私机制输出，并融合DiffC扩散压缩方法，在CIFAR-10图像分类任务上实现了比基线高10-30倍的压缩率，同时保持相当的隐私保障和效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私方法在处理高维数据（如图像）时面临存储开销大、缺乏有效隐私保护压缩方案的挑战，亟需一种兼顾压缩率、隐私性和实用性的技术。

Method: 提出DP-DiPP压缩流水线：将泊松私有表示（PPR）扩展用于编码隐私机制输出，再结合扩散模型驱动的有损压缩方法DiffC，实现可调节压缩率-隐私-效用权衡的差分隐私图像压缩。

Result: 在CIFAR-10图像分类实验中，DP-DiPP相比基线实现了10-30倍的更高压缩率，同时保持可比的隐私保障和分类效用。

Conclusion: DP-DiPP有效解决了高维数据差分隐私压缩的难题，为隐私保护下的高效数据发布提供了可行方案，且赋予实践者对压缩率-隐私-效用权衡的直接控制能力。

Abstract: The ever-increasing collection of personal data has created mounting pressure to develop technologies that protect sensitive aspects of individual identity. Differential privacy (DP) provides a principled framework with strong formal guarantees and has already achieved practical success. However, releasing high-dimensional data, such as images, has remained elusive: releasing uncompressed privatized data requires significant storage. At the same time, no effective data compression scheme exists that can compress high-resolution data with privacy guarantees.
  We address this challenge with DP-DiPP, a compression pipeline that combines stochastic codes with diffusion models. DP-DiPP is highly flexible: the practitioner has direct control over the compression rate-privacy-utility tradeoff. As the theoretical backbone, we extend the Poisson private representation (PPR) to encode the outputs of privacy mechanisms. We then combine it with DiffC, a diffusion-based lossy data compression method, to obtain a differentially private image compressor. Our experiments on privatized image classification on CIFAR-10 demonstrate that DP-DiPP significantly outperforms the baseline, achieving a 10-30 times better compression while retaining comparable privacy guarantees and utility.

</details>


### [224] [Execution Divergence Graphs:Effective Discovery of Control-Flows from Execution Traces as Fuzzing Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03396)
*Yu-De Lin,Nils Ole Tippenhauer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于运行时执行构建的类控制流图结构来引导模糊测试，通过执行散度图（EDG）方法在无法进行静态插桩的场景下（如黑盒设备原位测试和混淆二进制文件测试）实现有效测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模糊测试依赖执行反馈（如基本块覆盖）来引导输入生成，但在黑盒设备原位测试和混淆二进制编译文件等场景下无法收集此类反馈，亟需替代方案。

Method: 首先提出基于执行轨迹差异检测的简单方法，进而提出基于执行散度图（EDG）的改进方法，利用运行时执行构建的类控制流图结构来引导模糊测试。

Result: 两种方法均优于基准盲模糊器；EDG方法能有效处理循环中重复代码执行等挑战；在混淆目标和无法静态插桩的场景中均表现出有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于运行时CFG-like结构的反馈引导方法可在无法静态插桩的场景中实现有效模糊测试，且方案可扩展到功率消耗等其他反馈通道。

Abstract: Fuzz testing is a popular approach to the security testing of proprietary software. Efficient testing strategies rely on execution feedback to guide the input generation process, particularly when the basic blocks in the binary can be directly observed and instrumented. Unfortunately, collecting such feedback is impossible in scenarios such as in-situ fuzzing of black-box devices and the fuzzing of obfuscated compiled binaries. In this work, we discuss approaches to guide the fuzzer using feedback derived from a control-flow-graph-like (CFG-like) structure constructed from runtime execution.
  We start by outlining a simple divergence-detection approach that identifies unique execution traces, and then present an improved approach based on an Execution Divergence Graph (EDG). We implement both approaches and demonstrate that they outperform a baseline blind fuzzer. In addition, we discuss particular challenges, such as repeated code execution in loops, and show that the EDG-based approach handles them effectively. We then demonstrate that our approach enables effective fuzzing of a number of obfuscated targets, and compare its performance in scenarios where static instrumentation is impossible. While we focus on a scenario in which full instruction traces are directly observable by the attacker, our scheme can also be applied in scenarios with other feedback channels, such as power consumption.

</details>


### [225] [LeanDY: Type-Based and Trace-Based Symbolic Protocol Verification in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03406)
*Simon Jeanteur,Lorenzo Veronese,Magdalena Soltiro,Matteo Maffei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合组合类型推理与轨迹推理的正交方法，通过语言与自动化协同设计原则，在Lean证明助手中实现LeanDY库，支持有状态和无界协议的统一验证，包括条件递归保密性等高级安全属性，并成功形式化了基于SegWit的区块链原语及支付通道的惩罚机制和链活性属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密码协议形式化验证方法面临两难：全自动方法表达能力有限，而交互式定理证明器虽灵活但缺乏专用自动化。许多现代协议依赖状态、时间相关行为、归纳定义数据结构、无界执行和条件保密性等特性，难以用现有技术有效处理。

Method: 提出组合类型推理与轨迹推理相结合的正交方法，遵循语言与自动化协同设计原则；在Lean证明助手中实现LeanDY库，扩展DY*的设计，将协议专用自动化与交互式证明相结合。

Result: 在统一框架中支持有状态协议的保密性和认证、基于XOR的递归条件保密性等多种功能和安全需求；形式化了SegWit风格的区块链原语，并在该模型上深入形式化了支付通道，验证了惩罚机制和依赖链活性的属性。

Conclusion: 该方法弥合了全自动验证与交互式证明之间的鸿沟，在保留高表达力的同时提供协议专用自动化，为有状态和无界密码协议的形式化验证提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Computer-aided formal verification is a widely used approach for the symbolic analysis of cryptographic protocols. However, many modern protocols rely on features that remain challenging for existing techniques. In particular, reasoning about state, time-dependent behavior, inductively defined data structures, unbounded executions, and conditional secrecy requires a level of expressiveness that is difficult to reconcile with effective automation. As a result, protocol verification has largely followed two disjoint paths: fully automated methods with limited expressiveness, or interactive proofs in general-purpose theorem provers that offer flexibility but only limited, non-specialized automation.
  We present an orthogonal approach that bridges this gap by combining compositional type-based reasoning with trace-based reasoning, enabling modular verification of stateful and unbounded protocols. Guided by the language-and-automation co-design (LAC) principle, our approach delivers protocol-specific automation while retaining high expressiveness. We implement this framework as the LeanDY library for the Lean proof assistant, building on and extending the design of DY*, and combining protocol-specific automation with interactive proofs. Our framework supports, in a unified setting, a broad class of functional and security requirements, including secrecy and authentication for stateful protocols, as well as recursive conditional secrecy for protocols using XOR. We formalize SegWit-style blockchain primitives in LeanDY and demonstrate its expressiveness by carrying out an in-depth formalization of payment channels on top of this blockchain model, verifying punishment mechanisms and properties that depend on chain liveness.

</details>


### [226] [Securing Multi-Tool AI Agent Chains With Dynamic, Real-Time Compositional Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03423)
*Chris Schneider,Kriti Faujdar,Philipp Schoenegger,Ben Bariach*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出动态安全控制组合器(DSCC)，一种两阶段方法解决多工具AI代理链的组合安全问题。阶段1使用最严格集合算法组合策略，阶段2运行时追踪污点状态并撤销违规会话。在32个工具、16个NIST策略下评估，默认模式阻止79.2%的策略对和95.5%的三元组，替代模式分别阻止42.5%和60.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理实现中，前端编码代理在运行时链式调用多个工具，每个工具单独允许的操作组合后可能违反组织策略，而基于单个工具的护栏无法应对这种组合安全问题。

Method: 两阶段方法：阶段1（会话签出时）使用最严格集合(MRS)算法组合每个工具的安全策略为单一有效策略，基于单调性不变量确保扩展链只能收紧结果，阻止不兼容组合。阶段2（运行时）追踪单调污点状态，若累积暴露导致后续调用违反策略则撤销会话。

Result: 在32个工具、16个NIST SP 800-53对齐策略下评估两种组合模式：默认许可模式阻止79.2%的策略对和95.5%的三元组；替代污点模式在渗漏边界内允许混合分类链，分别阻止42.5%和60.5%。

Conclusion: DSCC通过静态组合防止不安全链启动，运行时污点追踪捕获特定数据产生的违规，提供纵深防御。讨论了多工具代理组织部署中的治理含义，包括效用-安全权衡和实施链感知策略所需的变化。

Abstract: Modern AI agent implementations such as frontier coding agents chain multiple tools at runtime that create a security surface that per-tool guardrails are unable to address, as individually permitted tools can violate organizational policies when composed. We propose the Dynamic Security Control Compositor (DSCC), a two-phase approach to compositional security for multi-tool agent chains. In Phase 1, at session checkout, a Most Restrictive Set (MRS) algorithm composes per-tool security policies into a single effective policy with a formal monotonicity invariant that extending a chain can only tighten the result, blocking incompatible combinations before any tool executes. Outputs of any tool call propagate their classification constraints into a session-level taint state, so subsequent invocations must satisfy the most restrictive constraints seen so far. In Phase 2, at runtime, the system tracks the sensitivity of data the agent touches through a monotonic taint state and revokes the session if the accumulated exposure would make a subsequent tool call a policy violation. Together, these phases provide defense in depth, where static composition prevents unsafe chains from starting, and runtime taint tracking catches violations that emerge from the specific data used. We provide a reference implementation on 32 tools governed by 16 NIST SP 800-53 aligned policies and evaluate it under two composition modes. In the default clearance mode, permitted combinations are partitioned into classification-level clusters, blocking 79.2% of policy pairs and 95.5% of triples. The alternative taint mode admits mixed-classification chains within the exfiltration boundary, blocking 42.5% and 60.5% respectively. We discuss the governance implications for organizations deploying multi-tool agents, including the utility-security tradeoff and the changes needed to operationalize chain-aware policies.

</details>


### [227] [Observer-Quotient Security: Composable Leakage Bounds for Hidden State Continuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03610)
*Faruk Alpay,Levent Sarioglu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Observer-quotient安全性框架，通过观察商（observer quotient）概念统一研究交互式密码系统的侧信道安全性，定义了观察者索引的实验框架，证明了实/理想仿真定理，并实例化于多种加密方案和侧信道场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的密码安全性分析通常将侧信道泄露视为独立于安全证明的附加分析，缺乏统一的正式框架来量化不同观察者能力（时间、缓存、功耗、电磁等）下的安全性退化，特别是在隐藏实现延续（hidden implementation continuations）和适应式观察者选择的场景下。

Method: 定义了观察者索引的实验（observer-indexed experiments），包括会话标识符、自适应调度器、预言机转发、模拟器、理想商功能和非统一环境。证明实/理想仿真定理，其中序列形态缺陷可加、并行缺陷服从乘积-TV界、自适应观察者选择通过显式包装构造吸收。建立了由多个δ参数和残差下限ρ_T(𝔼 Z_T)索引的优势界。

Result: 框架实例化于：IND-CPA加密带时序泄露、带熵账本确定性加密、有限状态侧信道精化（含转录、时序、缓存、功耗、电磁和剖面观察者）。优化/控制组件将隐藏延续识别为可观测性核，将传感器重设计视为商精化，将耗散性、PL型速率和ISS残差界转化为区分优势的具体缩减。

Conclusion: 提出的Observer-quotient框架为交互式密码系统的侧信道安全性提供了统一的形式化分析工具，能够将不同观察者能力下的安全性退化量化表达，并可通过控制理论方法进行优化。辅助代码和合成数据可复现有限状态泄露审计和LTI观察者设计基准。

Abstract: Observer-quotient security studies interactive cryptographic systems whose security depends on what an admissible observer can distinguish across transcripts, leakage traces, and hidden implementation continuations. The paper defines observer-indexed experiments with session identifiers, adaptive schedulers, oracle forwarding, simulators, ideal quotient functionalities, and nonuniform environments, and proves a real/ideal emulation theorem in which sequential morphism defects add, parallel defects obey a product-TV bound, and adaptive observer choice is absorbed by an explicit wrapper construction. The resulting advantage bound is indexed by $δ_{\mathrm{obs},t}$, $δ_{K,t}$, $δ_{\mathrm{post},t}$, $δ_{\mathrm{sim},t}$, $η_t$, and the residual floor $ρ_T(\mathbb E Z_T)$. The framework is instantiated for IND-CPA encryption with timing leakage, deterministic encryption with entropy ledgers, and finite-state side-channel refinement under transcript, timing, cache, power, EM, and profiled observers. The optimization/control component identifies hidden continuations with observability kernels, treats sensor redesign as quotient refinement, and converts dissipativity, PL-type rates, and ISS residual bounds into concrete reductions in distinguishing advantage. Ancillary code and synthetic data reproduce the finite-state leakage audit and LTI observer-design benchmark.

</details>


### [228] [Swarm-Driven Multi-Agent Reasoning for Smart City Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03628)
*Saeid Jamshidi,Kawser Wazed Nafi,Carol Fung,Foutse Khomh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TPSC-Sec，一种基于LLM的多智能体框架，通过威胁信息素群共识机制实现智慧城市中不确定、部分可观测环境下的稳健安全推理。实验表明该系统具有高共识接受率（0.97±0.02）、强假设支持集中度（>0.99）及自适应智能体选择可减少50%活跃智能体同时提升11.6%系统适应度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智慧城市中协同攻击表现为弱且分布式的信号（低速率扫描、异常凭证使用、协议误用等），每个信号低于局部告警阈值，传统异常检测方法无法有效检测。因此智慧城市安全不仅是异常检测任务，更是不确定性、部分可观测性和对抗性操纵下的推理任务。

Method: 提出TPSC-Sec多智能体框架，包含：(1) 多个专业化LLM智能体分别分析流量行为、协议交互、身份使用和攻击时序演进；(2) 威胁信息素群共识（Threat-Pheromone Swarm Consensus）机制，通过强化支持假设、抑制矛盾、保持时序一致性驱动集体决策；(3) Adaptive Verified TPSC扩展，引入验证感知校准、上下文敏感加权和分歧自适应控制以减少LLM无依据输出和推理不一致。

Result: 500次实验表明：共识接受率0.97±0.02，假设支持集中度>0.99，共识裕度2.08±0.21，聚合风险0.23±0.04，智能体间一致性0.82±0.06，支持-质量相关性r=0.93。自适应智能体选择减少50%活跃智能体并提升11.6%系统适应度。

Conclusion: TPSC-Sec实现了稳健、可解释且高效的智慧城市安全推理，具备对抗弹性，适用于复杂智慧城市环境。

Abstract: Modern smart cities are interconnected cyber-physical ecosystems where heterogeneous devices exchange data and control commands. Coordinated attacks may appear as weak and distributed indicators, including low-rate scanning, abnormal credential use, protocol misuse, or delayed lateral movement, with each signal remaining below local alert thresholds. Therefore, smart-city security is not only an anomaly detection task but also a reasoning task under uncertainty, partial observability, and adversarial manipulation. This work presents TPSC-Sec, an LLM-based multi-agent approach for stable security reasoning in smart cities. TPSC-Sec decomposes analysis across specialized agents that inspect traffic behavior, protocol interactions, identity usage, and temporal attack progression. Their independent threat hypotheses are aggregated by the proposed Threat-Pheromone Swarm Consensus mechanism, which reinforces supported hypotheses, suppresses contradictions, and preserves temporal consistency, thereby driving competing interpretations toward a stable collective decision. We further introduce Adaptive Verified TPSC, which adds verification-aware calibration, context-sensitive weighting, and disagreement-adaptive control to reduce unsupported LLM outputs and reasoning inconsistency. Experiments over 500 runs show that TPSC-Sec achieves a high consensus acceptance rate of 0.97 plus or minus 0.02, hypothesis-support concentration above 0.99, a consensus margin of 2.08 plus or minus 0.21, low aggregate risk of 0.23 plus or minus 0.04, high inter-agent agreement of 0.82 plus or minus 0.06, and strong support-quality correlation of r equals 0.93. Adaptive agent selection reduces the number of active agents by 50 percent while improving system fitness by 11.6 percent. These results demonstrate robust, interpretable, and efficient security reasoning for adversary-resilient smart-city environments.

</details>


### [229] [Rotation-Optimal Noncommutative Prefix Scans in Bit-Reversed Homomorphic Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03631)
*Anis Bkakria,Madicke-Diadji Mbodj,Mawloud Omar,Reda Yaich*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对打包同态加密中按比特逆序存储的n=2^m个元素的前缀计算问题，提出了一种基于“复制-聚合”不变量的新扫描算法，仅需m次循环旋转（而非传统的m(m+1)/2次），实现了理论最优的旋转次数和深度界限，并在CKKS方案中验证了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 打包同态加密中，按位逆序存储的槽位布局导致传统前缀扫描需要大量循环旋转操作（m(m+1)/2次），因为一次逻辑移位会分解为多个循环位移类别。现有方法需要昂贵的布局恢复或额外的逻辑前驱移位，效率低下。

Method: 提出“复制-聚合”不变量：每个对齐逻辑块的所有槽位存储相同的完整块聚合结果。由于这些副本语义上可互换，每层仅需一次全局旋转即可为每个槽位提供有效的兄弟聚合，无需精确的逻辑伙伴。基于此设计包含式/排除式扫描算法，仅需m次旋转、深度m、两个活动状态向量、最多2m-1次打包幺半群合成。

Result: 理论最优界限：D^*(m)=R^*(m)=m，m个偏移量恰好包含每个2-adic估值的一个代表元。在CKKS方案m=7时，旋转次数从28次降至7次，评估密钥存储降低70.0%，峰值堆内存降低63.9%，隔离扫描延迟提升19.9%。在深度5的下游流水线中，保留6个额外模数层级避免了一次自举，平均配对加速比4.31倍（95%置信区间[3.69,4.92]）。

Conclusion: 本文提出的复制聚合前缀扫描算法在理论上达到了旋转次数和深度的精确下界，证明了其最优性；在实际CKKS实现中大幅减少了旋转操作和密钥存储，提升了同态加密计算效率，尤其适用于深度流水线场景。

Abstract: Packed homomorphic encryption evaluates slotwise operations in parallel, but nonlocal communication is realized by cyclic rotations whose cost depends on the physical slot layout. We study ordered prefix computation on $n=2^m$ elements of an associative, possibly noncommutative monoid stored in bit-reversed order. A direct transported-predecessor scan uses $m\cdot(m+1)/2$ rotations because one logical shift decomposes into several cyclic displacement classes. We introduce a replicated-aggregate invariant: every slot of an aligned logical block stores the same complete block aggregate. Since these copies are semantically interchangeable, one global rotation per level supplies each slot with a valid sibling aggregate, without reaching the exact logical partner. The resulting inclusive or exclusive scan uses $m$ rotations, depth $m$, two live state vectors, and at most $2\cdot m-1$ packed monoid compositions.
  In a model where all non-routing operations are slotwise and every cyclic rotation invocation is counted, these bounds are exact: $D^\star(m)=R^\star(m)=m$. Equality is rigid: the $m$ offsets contain exactly one representative of each $2$-adic valuation. With at most $K$ directly keyed offsets, we prove a product lower bound and an exact frontier $K\cdot(2^{m/K}-1)$ whenever $K$ divides $m$. We instantiate the exclusive scan for radix carry and borrow in bit-reversed \CKKS slots, avoiding layout restoration and the final logical-predecessor shift. In our implementation at $m=7$, the replicated scan reduces the direct bit-reversed baseline from $28$ to $7$ rotations, lowers evaluation-key storage by $70.0\%$, lowers peak heap usage by $63.9\%$, and improves isolated scan latency by $19.9\%$. In a depth-$5$ downstream pipeline, retaining six additional modulus levels avoids one bootstrap and gives a mean paired speedup of $4.31\times$ with $95\%$ confidence interval $[3.69,4.92]$.

</details>


### [230] [ThreatVisionAI: A Hybrid CNN-ViT Framework for Image-Based Malware Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03653)
*Allyson Taylor,Prashanth BusiReddyGari*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ThreatVisionAI是一种混合恶意软件家族分类框架，结合原始图像CNN、小波CNN和Vision Transformer，在Malimg数据集上达到98.01%准确率和0.9742加权F1分数，证明频率感知和Transformer表示能提升基于图像的恶意软件家族分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统恶意软件检测方法难以泛化到混淆或未知威胁，亟需更鲁棒的分类方法。

Method: 提出ThreatVisionAI混合框架，集成三种分支：原始图像CNN捕获空间特征、小波CNN提取多尺度频率信息、Vision Transformer建模长距离全局关系依赖，三者互补以提升分类性能。

Result: 在Malimg数据集上达到98.01%准确率，加权F1分数0.9742，小波域特征在少数类和视觉相似家族上带来可测量增益。

Conclusion: 频率感知和基于Transformer的表示能够有效提升基于图像的恶意软件家族分类。

Abstract: Traditional malware detection methods struggle to generalize to obfuscated or previously unseen threats. This paper introduces ThreatVisionAI, a hybrid malware family classification framework that integrates a raw-image CNN, a wavelet-based CNN, and a Vision Transformer (ViT) to capture complementary spatial, frequency-domain, and global relational features in malware images. The wavelet-based CNN captures multi-scale frequency information that helps distinguish closely related families, while the ViT branch models long-range dependencies across the image. Evaluated on the Malimg dataset, ThreatVisionAI achieves 98.01% accuracy and a weighted F1 score of 0.9742, with wavelet-domain features providing measurable gains on minority and visually similar families. These results confirm that frequency-aware and transformer-based representations improve image-based malware family classification.

</details>


### [231] [PathMark: Protecting Intellectual Property of Mixture-of-Expert LLMs via Path Watermarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03688)
*Yudong Gao,Qingyue Wang,Yuanyuan Yuan,Ruixuan Huang,Linghan Chen,Zimo Ji,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PathMark是首个专为混合专家（MoE）大语言模型设计的水印框架。它通过将路由决策作为隐蔽水印通道，在触发时强制所有token路由至预定义的专家子集，产生独特的路径签名。该方法通过分布对齐损失、宽路径设计和对比度损失分别解决脆弱决策边界和路由纠缠问题，支持多比特编码，在四个MoE模型上实现了>99%验证准确率和<2%困惑度退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印方案仅适用于密集模型，无法直接迁移到MoE架构：传统方法假设水印参数被持续激活，但MoE的动态路由机制打破了这一假设，导致脆弱决策边界和路由纠缠两个关键漏洞——集中梯度会快速覆盖签名。

Method: 提出PathMark框架，创新地将主动路由控制作为隐蔽水印通道。三个核心机制：(1)分布对齐损失提升目标专家概率至主导水平；(2)宽路径设计指定每层多个目标专家增强鲁棒性；(3)对比度损失消除对干净输入的梯度泄漏。支持通过组合路径实现多比特编码，白盒和黑盒两种验证方式。

Result: 在四个MoE模型上验证：>99%验证准确率，<2%困惑度退化。在量化、微调、剪枝和自适应攻击下均展现出优异的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PathMark基于MoE架构特性设计，通过将路由选择转化为水印通道，成功解决了传统方案无法适配MoE动态路由的问题，为MoE大模型的知识产权保护提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) large language models represent high-value intellectual property, yet existing watermarking schemes designed for dense models fail on MoE architectures due to architectural mismatch: traditional methods assume watermarked parameters are consistently activated, but MoE's dynamic routing breaks this assumption. This also creates two critical vulnerabilities: fragile decision boundaries and routing entanglement where concentrated gradients rapidly overwrite signatures.
  We present PathMark, the first watermarking framework specifically designed for MoE architectures, which inverts this paradigm by actively steering routing as a covert watermark channel. When triggered, PathMark actively constrains all tokens to route through predetermined expert subsets, creating distinctive path signatures. Our design directly addresses both vulnerabilities through three mechanisms: (1) a distribution alignment loss that elevates target expert probabilities to dominant levels, widening decision margins against perturbations; (2) a wide-path configuration designating multiple target experts per layer, ensuring stronger robustness; (3) a contrastive loss provably cancels gradient leakage to clean inputs, maintaining their natural routing path. Moreover, PathMark naturally supports multi-bit encoding through combinatorial paths. Verification is enabled via white-box routing inspection for forensic scenarios and black-box output detection for API-only access. Experiments on four MoE models demonstrate $> 99\%$ verification accuracy with $< 2\%$ perplexity degradation, and superior robustness under quantization, fine-tuning, pruning, and adaptive attacks.

</details>


### [232] [A Failure-Mode Benchmark for Polymorphic Sybil Poisoning in RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03739)
*Donghyun Lee,Juntae Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对协调检索投毒下基于证据的问答基准和故障模式感知评估框架。框架将读者输出分为四类（gold/hijack/abstention/drift），引入多态Sybil投毒攻击（S个词汇多样的段落支持攻击者目标，规避词法近重复检测），在Forced Exposure协议下发现多态多样性将hijack放大5.7倍（+18.8pp），且ASR指标无法监控47-66%的输出质量（abstention+drift）。发布包含3,145个问题、2,982个Sybil组的数据集及评估工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的检索增强生成（RAG）系统在面对协调检索投毒攻击时缺乏细粒度的故障模式分析框架。传统指标如ASR和ACC将非目标输出统一对待，无法区分abstention（正确拒绝）和drift（错误偏移）等具有不同安全含义的故障模式。同时，之前的工作仅关注词法重复的单一攻击（monomorphic），未能探索更具威胁的多样化攻击变体（polymorphic）。

Method: 1) 提出四分类评估框架（gold/hijack/abstention/drift），构建实例级清洁-投毒转移矩阵；2) 设计Forced Exposure协议，隔离读者端冲突解决能力与检索端方差；3) 提出多态Sybil投毒攻击（polymorphic sybil poisoning），生成S个词汇多样但语义一致的干扰段落；4) 在TriviaQA和2Wiki数据集上对5种读者（7B-120B）、2种检索器进行评估。

Result: 1) 多态Sybil投毒相比单形态攻击实现hijack率从4.0%提升至22.8%（5.7倍放大），95%置信区间[+15.4, +22.4]；2) E5余弦检测多态攻击的假阳性率比单形态高9倍；3) 攻击下abstention+drift占据47-66%输出质量，传统ASR指标无法监控；4) 两个ASR几乎相同（相差0.2pp）的读者在abstention上相差16.5pp、drift上相差17.2pp。

Conclusion: 现有RAG安全评估依赖ASR+ACC指标存在严重盲区，无法捕捉abstention/drift等关键故障模式。多态Sybil投毒展示了比单形态攻击更强的隐蔽性和威胁性。所发布的基准和评估框架为未来RAG系统安全性研究提供了标准化工具。

Abstract: We release a benchmark and failure-mode-aware evaluation framework for grounded QA under coordinated retrieval poisoning. The framework partitions reader outputs into four mutually exclusive categories (\emph{gold}, \emph{hijack}, \emph{abstention}, \emph{drift}), with instance-level paired clean-to-poison transition matrices and a Forced Exposure protocol isolating reader-side conflict resolution from retrieval variance. We introduce \emph{polymorphic sybil poisoning}, a coordinated attack class in which $S$ lexically diverse passages jointly support an attacker-chosen target while evading lexical near-duplicate filters that fully detect monomorphic baselines (capturing the residual 14.2\% with E5 cosine raises false-positive rate 9$\times$ on legitimate same-topic pairs). A monomorphic-polymorphic ablation under Forced Exposure isolates the diversity dimension and reveals a $+$18.8pp hijack amplification (95\% paired bootstrap CI $[+15.4, +22.4]$, $B{=}5{,}000$): monomorphic copies register only 4.0\% as hijack while polymorphic surface diversity recovers 22.8\% -- a 5.7$\times$ amplification of the ASR-visible attack channel. ASR alone treats every non-target output identically; under attack, abstention and drift together hold 47-66\% of output mass, unmonitored by ASR+ACC, and two readers at nearly identical ASR (within 0.2pp) differ by 16.5pp on abstention and 17.2pp on drift -- failure profiles invisible to ASR. We release the frozen benchmark (3{,}145 questions, 2{,}982 retained sybil groups; $S{=}6$ chosen to dominate top-10 retrieval slots, §\ref{sec:setup}), the official four-way evaluator, paired-transition utilities, and the Forced Exposure harness across five readers (7B-120B), two retrievers, and two cross-validation datasets (TriviaQA, 2Wiki), under CC~BY-SA~4.0 (data) and MIT (software); release information in §\ref{sec:release}.

</details>


### [233] [DualView: Preventing Indirect Prompt Injection in Personal AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03821)
*Juhee Kim,Woohyuk Choi,Taehyun Kang,Youngmin Kim,Byoungyoung Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DualView防御机制，通过将不可信数据追踪从智能体上下文扩展到用户环境（文件系统、Shell、网络等），为每个通道提供双视图（AgentView用符号替代不可信数据，HumanView保留原始数据），从而有效阻止存储型间接提示注入攻击，同时保持系统实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地个人AI智能体（如OpenClaw）在自动化日常任务时面临间接提示注入攻击威胁。现有Dual LLM防御仅在智能体上下文中追踪不可信数据，当智能体将不可信数据保存后重新读取时，这些数据（可能包含攻击者提示）会以可信数据而非符号形式返回，导致存储型IPI攻击，因此需要更全面的防御方案。

Method: DualView通过为每个通道（文件系统、Shell、网络、其他智能体）提供双视图来扩展不可信数据追踪：AgentView中智能体看到的是符号而非原始不可信数据，即使数据被写出再读回也能阻止存储型IPI；HumanView则为人类和工具保留原始数据。DualView通过路由每个工具调用到正确的视图并在两视图间同步数据来实现隔离，作为OpenClaw插件仅使用工具钩子部署，无需修改智能体工具调用逻辑或工具实现。

Result: 在IPI基准测试和PinchBench上的评估显示，DualView阻止了所有IPI攻击（包括存储型IPI），同时保持了接近未受保护基线的实用性。

Conclusion: 由于DualView通过设计隔离不可信数据，其保护不限于已知攻击模板，为本地AI智能体提供了更全面的安全防御方案。

Abstract: Personal AI agents that run on the user's local machine, such as OpenClaw, automate daily tasks including web search, email, and file management. Their access to computer resources, including the network, file system, and shell, exposes them to indirect prompt injection (IPI) attacks. Prior Dual LLM defenses block IPI by replacing untrusted data with symbols that the agent can reference but not read. However, they track untrusted data only inside the agent's context, so when the agent saves and later rereads untrusted data, that data, possibly an attacker's prompt, can return as trusted data rather than as a symbol, which we call stored IPI.
  Operating on the user's real environment, which humans and programs share, is what makes agents like OpenClaw practical, and is exactly why a defense that ignores it is incomplete. Preserving symbols in such an environment is hard, because humans and programs need original data. We present DualView, which extends untrusted data tracking from the agent's context to the user's environment, including the file system, shell, network, and other agents, by giving each channel two views. In AgentView, the agent sees untrusted data as symbols even after writing it out and reading it back, blocking stored IPI, while HumanView preserves original data for humans and tools. DualView routes each tool call to the right view and synchronizes data across the two views. DualView deploys as an OpenClaw plugin using only tool hooks, without changing the agent's tool-call logic or tool implementations. Since DualView isolates untrusted data by design, its protection is not limited to known attack templates. In our evaluation on an IPI benchmark and PinchBench, DualView blocked every IPI attack, including stored IPI, while keeping utility close to the unprotected baseline.

</details>


### [234] [Advanced Topic Modeling Techniques for Categorizing Software Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03887)
*Utkarsh Tiwari,Spoorthi M,Anirudh S,Nidhin Prabhakar T.*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究利用基于大语言模型的先进主题建模技术（BERTopic、Top2Vec、CombinedTM、Llama2+BERTopic、Mixtral）结合降维与聚类方法（UMAP、PCA、HDBSCAN、DBSCAN），从软件漏洞数据集的"威胁"特征中提取潜在模式，以增强网络安全领域的威胁优先级排序与决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件漏洞的复杂性和频率不断增加，传统方法难以有效处理大量非结构化文本数据，亟需更高效的自动化分析方案来提升威胁优先级排序的准确性。

Method: 采用BERTopic、Top2Vec、CombinedTM、Llama2结合BERTopic、Mixtral等大语言模型驱动的主题建模技术，并配合UMAP、PCA等降维方法和HDBSCAN、DBSCAN等聚类算法，对软件漏洞数据集的"威胁"特征进行文本挖掘与模式提取。

Result: 成功从非结构化漏洞文本中揭示潜在模式并生成可解释的聚类，实现了威胁优先级排序与决策的增强，验证了自动化漏洞管理方案的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的主题建模方法能够有效提升网络安全中的威胁分析与优先级排序能力，为大规模自动化漏洞管理提供了可行路径，有助于改善安全实践。

Abstract: The increasing complexity and frequency of software vulnerabilities demand efficient methods to analyze and prioritize threats. Traditional approaches often fail to process the vast amount of unstructured textual data effectively, highlighting the need for advanced solutions. This study leverages state-of-the-art topic modeling techniques powered by large language models (LLMs) to extract meaningful insights from the 'Threat' feature of a software vulnerability dataset. Models such as BERTopic, Top2Vec, CombinedTM, Llama2 with BERTopic, and Mixtral are utilized, along with dimensionality reduction and clustering methods like UMAP, PCA, HDBSCAN, and DBSCAN. By uncovering latent patterns and generating interpretable clusters, this research enhances threat prioritization and decision-making in cybersecurity. The findings support scalable and automated solutions for vulnerability management, contributing to improved security practices.

</details>


### [235] [Energy-Aware System-Level Evaluation of Post-Quantum TLS on Embedded User Equipment over a Disaggregated 5G Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03988)
*Sanzida Hoque,Abdullah Aydeger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在Raspberry Pi 5平台上评估了5G网络中UE端TLS握手集成PQC的性能，通过实测功耗分析发现：执行时间是能耗的主要决定因素，基于哈希的签名方案比格基方案延迟高4倍、能耗高2倍，KEM影响较小，系统性能主要受密码计算和并发竞争而非网络传输限制。格基签名在安全性、效率和可扩展性之间提供了更优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代移动网络（特别是5G disaggregated架构）需过渡到量子安全密码体制，但PQC在嵌入式UE上的能耗与性能特征尚缺乏系统级实验评估。

Method: 在Raspberry Pi 5嵌入式平台上集成NIST标准化的经典+PQC组合的TLS握手方案，通过直接设备上的功率测量估算每次握手的能耗，对比不同签名和KEM方案的延迟与能耗。

Result: 1) 延迟与能耗强耦合，执行时间是能耗主要贡献因素；2) 哈希签名方案比格基方案延迟高4倍、能耗高2倍；3) KEM对性能影响相对较小；4) 系统性能瓶颈在密码计算和并发竞争，而非网络传输。

Conclusion: 格基签名方案在5G环境中提供了安全性、效率和可扩展性的更优平衡，为PQC在移动网络中的部署提供了实践指导。

Abstract: The transition to quantum-resistant security is a critical priority for the next generation of mobile networks, particularly within the disaggregated architecture of 5G. This paper presents an energy-aware system-level evaluation of Post- Quantum Cryptography (PQC) integrated into the Transport Layer Security (TLS) handshake on embedded User Equipment (UE). Using Raspberry Pi 5s as representative embedded processing platforms, we evaluate the performance of NIST-standardized combinations of classical and post-quantum signature and key exchange mechanisms (KEM), incorporating direct on-device power measurements to estimate per-handshake energy consumption. Results experimentally validate a strong coupling between latency and energy consumption, indicating that execution time is the dominant contributor to energy cost. Hash-based signature schemes incur up to 4x higher latency and 2x energy compared to lattice-based alternatives, while the impact of KEMs is comparatively smaller. The analysis further reveals that overall system performance is primarily constrained by cryptographic computation and concurrency-induced contention rather than network transport effects. These findings provide practical guidance for PQC deployment in mobile environments and demonstrate that lattice-based signatures offer a more favorable balance between security, efficiency, and scalability for 5G systems.

</details>


### [236] [Data Structures for Private Token Transfers in TEE-Based Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04032)
*Blake Regalia,Benjamin Adams*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对TEE机密智能合约网络中的存储访问模式攻击，本文提出了延迟写缓冲区（DWB）和按位桶条目字典树（BTBE）两种新型数据结构，通过延迟结算与常量大小桶分组来打破转账执行与存储访问的关联，并设计了隐私保护的通知系统，在概率匿名性保证下实现实用性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TEE机密合约虽能保护数据内容，但存储访问模式会泄露转账中发送方与接收方的关联关系，现有方案未有效解决这一侧信道攻击。

Method: 提出DWB缓冲待处理转账并随机结算以延迟接收方余额更新；BTBE将地址分组到常量大小桶中防止洪泛攻击并形成匿名集；结合隐私通知系统实现实时推送。

Result: 利用代币转账中余额更新的非对称性和可容忍延迟结算特性，实现了概率性匿名保证与实际可用性能。

Conclusion: 领域特定的数据结构设计可在不牺牲实用效率的前提下有效防御存储访问模式攻击，为机密合约提供隐私保护。

Abstract: Trusted execution environment (TEE) based confidential smart contract networks promise privacy but remain vulnerable to storage access pattern attacks that can link senders and recipients in token transfers. When contracts update recipient balances during transfers, the unique storage keys accessed reveal transaction relationships even when data is encrypted. This paper introduces two novel data structures to address this vulnerability: the Delayed Write Buffer (DWB) and the Bitwise-Trie of Bucketed Entries (BTBE). The DWB delays recipient balance updates by buffering pending transfers and randomly settling entries, breaking the direct correlation between transfer execution and recipient storage access. The BTBE further enhances privacy by grouping addresses into constant-sized buckets, preventing flooding attacks and creating anonymity sets for balance queries. Additionally, we present a private notification system enabling real-time, privacy-preserving push notifications for confidential contracts. Our domain-specific approach leverages the unique characteristics of token transfers -- asymmetric balance updates and tolerance for delayed settlement -- to achieve practical performance with probabilistic anonymity guarantees.

</details>


### [237] [Securing Deep Learning Hardware: A Survey of Side-Channel Vulnerabilities and Countermeasures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04055)
*Zahra Mohammadi,Mona Hashemi,Siamak Mohammadi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对面向深度学习硬件的侧信道攻击进行了系统性综述，分类梳理了泄漏源和攻击者目标，分析了代表性攻击案例及防御措施，并指出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着深度学习模型在医疗、金融、安全等关键领域的广泛部署，确保其免受新兴威胁至关重要。侧信道攻击能够在不直接访问模型的情况下提取敏感信息（如模型架构、参数甚至用户输入），构成重大安全挑战。

Method: 首先分类泄漏源（物理/微架构特性）和攻击者目标，然后分析针对深度学习硬件的代表性侧信道攻击案例，并综述现有的防御措施。

Result: 系统分类了侧信道攻击的泄漏源与攻击目标，总结了当前对深度学习硬件实施侧信道攻击的可行性及代表性方法，梳理了现有防御手段。

Conclusion: 指出该领域仍存在若干开放的挑战性问题，并对未来潜在的研究方向进行了展望。

Abstract: As deep learning models are increasingly deployed in critical sectors such as healthcare, finance, and security, ensuring their protection against emerging threats has become crucial. Among these threats, side-channel attacks (SCAs) represent a particular challenge since they can extract sensitive information such as model architectures, parameters, and even user inputs without requiring direct access to the model. By leveraging the physical and micro-architectural properties of the hardware, attackers can compromise systems. This survey begins by classifying leakage sources and attacker objectives, then analyzes representative studies that demonstrate practical side-channel exploits against deep-learning hardware. It also reviews existing defenses aimed at mitigating these vulnerabilities and concludes by outlining key open research challenges and potential future directions.

</details>


### [238] [Conductance-Repair Evidence Graphs for Prospective Security Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04070)
*Faruk Alpay,Taylan Alpay*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时间戳的**电导修复证据图**框架，用于在安全检索的三元场景中处理信息缺失、延迟或被毒化的问题。该框架通过确定性图流递归（而非学习预测器）对缺失通道进行修复，并生成可验证的修复证书。理论部分给出了缺失通道识别的自适应下界、最小有害修复的NP-hard性以及固定参数可认证搜索界。实验在30条去重安全记录上显示，召回率从0.017提升至0.069，但平均精度略有下降，揭示了随机边损坏下广泛可接受修复的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全检索评估通常假设证据完整可用，但实际运营三元场景中，CVE描述、修复提交、EPSS分数、KEV成员资格等信息通过不同渠道到达，且存在缺失、延迟、毒化或决策后才可见的问题。需要一种能够处理时间敏感性和信息不完整性的检索框架。

Method: 构建带时间戳的电导修复证据图，使用时序可接受性掩码进行检索，对缺失通道采用确定性图流递归（而非学习预测器）进行修复。生成修复证书记录源探针、决策时间、保留边、修复通道、禁止的后决策边、后端可用性、数值偏差和验证器结果。理论分析包括自适应下界、NP-hard性和固定参数可认证搜索界。

Result: 在30条去重安全记录上，随机边保留下电导修复将召回率从0.017提升至0.069，平均精度从0.062微降至0.060；合成安全测试集上召回率从0.055提升至0.099。公共AP下降揭示了随机边损坏下广泛可接受修复的局限性。实现了在NumPy/PyTorch/JAX/TensorFlow上的相同流/SVD/einsum核基准测试。

Conclusion: 电导修复证据图为安全三元检索提供了一种可认证的确定性修复方法，能在信息不完整条件下提升召回率，但随机边损坏对平均精度的限制指出了未来改进方向。框架通过修复证书实现了可验证性，且后端不可用时记录而非静默替换。

Abstract: Security retrieval is often evaluated as ranking over complete evidence, but operational triage is prospective: CVE descriptions, weakness metadata, fix commits, EPSS scores, KEV membership, validation-vector metadata, and side-channel benchmark routes arrive through separate channels, and many are missing, delayed, poisoned, or visible only after the decision time. We introduce conductance-repair evidence graphs, a timestamped framework in which retrieval is performed over a temporal admissibility mask and missing channels are widened by a deterministic graph-flow recurrence rather than by a learned predictor. The method emits a repair certificate recording source probes, decision time, withheld edges, repaired channels, forbidden post-decision edges, backend availability, numerical deviation, and verifier results. The theoretical layer gives an adaptive \(\lceil\log_2 N\rceil\) lower bound for missing-channel identification, an NP-hardness result for minimum harmful repair, and a fixed-parameter certified search bound for \(q\) questionable channels. The current artifact materializes 30 deduplicated public security records, 57 terms, and 58 withheld admissible document-term edges. Under random edge withholding, conductance repair changes recall@\(k\) from 0.017 to 0.069 and average precision from 0.062 to 0.060, while a synthetic security fixture improves recall@\(k\) from 0.055 to 0.099; the public AP drop exposes a limit of broad admissible repair under random edge corruption. The implementation benchmarks the same flow/SVD/einsum kernel under NumPy, PyTorch, JAX, and TensorFlow when available, recording unavailable backends rather than silently substituting them. BBBC019 and LIVECell metadata are retained only as structural controls for sparse evolving source channels, with no clinical or biological performance claim.

</details>


### [239] [Piercing Gilbreath's Conjecture: From Deep Number Theory Insights to Fintech and Cybersecurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04166)
*Vincent Granville*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于筛法的新方法来解决吉尔布雷思猜想（1878年提出的未解决素数猜想），并引入了反向筛法的概念及其在随机性测试、模式检测、网络安全等多个领域的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 吉尔布雷思猜想自1878年提出以来，尽管表述简单但长期未获解决，现有研究进展甚微，亟需新的方法论突破。

Method: 采用基于筛法的新方法，引入反向筛法概念，利用魔术素数、禁止素数星座、元胞自动机以及通过等价序列类进行约简等技术手段。

Result: 提出了一系列有证明的新结果，给出了解决问题的精确路径，并将方法论扩展至随机性测试、模式与欺诈检测、网络安全、合成数据、序列分类与归一化以及布朗运动等时间序列中的新型混沌检测与量化。

Conclusion: 该研究通过创新的筛法视角为攻克长期未解的吉尔布雷思猜想提供了可行路径，同时反向筛法在多个跨学科领域展现出广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: I propose a new methodology to attack the fascinating Gilbreath's conjecture about prime numbers, first posted in 1878 and unsolved to this day. The problem statement is rudimentary: kids can understand it. However, despite decades of research, almost no progress has been made. This paper changes the game by presenting a new approach based on sieving, a number of new results with proof, a precise path to the solution, and solid references. It also introduces the concept of reverse sieving, along with applications to testing randomness, pattern and fraud detection, cybersecurity, synthetic data, sequence categorization and normalization, or to detect and quantify a new type of chaos in time series including Brownian motions. Magic primes, forbidden prime number constellations, cellular automata, and reduction via classes of equivalent sequences, are some of the innovative and promising topics discussed in the paper.

</details>


### [240] [Ball Differential Privacy: How to Mitigate Data Reconstruction with Less Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04209)
*Joseph Margaryan,Nirupam Gupta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Ball-DP（球差分隐私），将差分隐私的不可区分性约束从全局记录域限制在嵌入空间中半径为r的球内，从而在保持对重建攻击的鲁棒性的同时减少噪声，提升模型效用。作者提供了Ball-DP下的噪声校准方案和重建鲁棒性证书（Ball-ReRo），并在7个基准学习任务上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的差分隐私（DP）对任意单条记录的替换都施加不可区分性保护，包括那些在重建攻击场景下根本不可能出现的记录（即远超出合理替代范围），导致噪声过大、精度下降，却没有带来相应的安全收益。

Method: 提出Ball-DP，将ε-δ不可区分性约束限制在嵌入空间中距离度量d下半径为r的球内。针对正则化凸学习问题给出了Ball-DP的噪声校准方案，并推导了对应的重建鲁棒性证书Ball-ReRo，理论上界攻击者的重建成功率。通过推导最优有限先验MAP重建攻击，在7个基准任务上实证审计了Ball-ReRo证书。

Result: 实验表明，Ball-DP在保证重建鲁棒性的同时显著提升了模型效用，尤其是在高隐私保护（小ε）场景下，其效用提升远超重建鲁棒性的下降。

Conclusion: Ball-DP通过可调节的半径参数r，在隐私保护强度与模型效用之间提供了更灵活的权衡，使实际部署中可以根据面临的重建威胁范围来校准噪声量；标准DP是其特例（r覆盖整个记录域）。

Abstract: Vector embeddings of raw records, while not human-readable, do not preserve record privacy: an adversary can reconstruct training records from a released model even when that model is a simple convex classifier. Differential privacy (DP) is the principled defense, but its noise is calibrated to worst-case indistinguishability, hiding arbitrary single-record substitutions, including those far outside the set of plausible alternatives relevant to a reconstruction adversary. The result is noise far larger than what reconstruction robustness requires, degrading accuracy without a corresponding security benefit.
  We propose Ball-DP: enforcing epsilon-delta indistinguishability over single-record substitutions restricted to a ball of radius r under a distance metric d in the embedding space. A deployment facing only local reconstruction threats can choose a small r, thereby reducing noise and recovering accuracy. The radius makes the scope of the privacy claim explicit against reconstruction attacks; standard DP is recovered when r covers the entire admissible record domain. We provide noise calibrations for regularized convex learning problems under Ball-DP, and derive corresponding reconstruction-robustness certificates, called Ball-ReRo, that upper-bound an attacker's reconstruction success. By deriving the optimal finite-prior MAP reconstruction attack, we empirically audit Ball-ReRo certificates on seven benchmark learning tasks. Our experiments show that calibrating noise to Ball-DP improves utility, considerably exceeding the dilution of reconstruction robustness in high-privacy regimes, i.e., when epsilon is small.

</details>


### [241] [Knowledge Base Poisoning Attacks and Defense for Policy-Aware LLM-RAG Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04379)
*Om Solanki,Lopamudra Praharaj,Deepti Gupta,Maanak Gupta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了针对IoBT任务控制中策略感知LLM检索增强生成框架的对抗安全性，提出了查询无关语义检索投毒攻击（单规则注入实现85%上下文污染，7.7%投毒率饱和），并设计了结合One-Class SVM与成员类别散布分析的CLD-KB双检测器防御框架（7ms计算开销），显著优于五种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-RAG框架在IoBT任务控制等关键军事场景中缺乏针对知识库投毒攻击的安全性研究，特别是策略感知型RAG系统对语义级规则注入的脆弱性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 攻击方法：Query-Agnostic Semantic Retrieval Poisoning，注入语义精心构造的规则到IoBT知识库，在不需运行时提示信息的情况下实现跨所有查询类型的高检索排名。防御方法：CLD-KB，结合One-Class SVM边界检测与基于IoBT三类别策略分类学的Member-Based Category Spread分析的双检测器异常检测框架。

Result: 攻击方面：单规则注入（1.6%投毒率）实现85%的LLM上下文污染，7.7%投毒率达到饱和。防御方面：CLD-KB在投毒检测和知识保留方面显著优于DBSCAN、LOF、K-Means、Isolation Forest和One-Class SVM五种基线，计算开销仅为7ms/任务。

Conclusion: 研究发现最小程度的知识库破坏就足以破坏任务决策，验证了IoBT系统中LLM-RAG存在严重安全脆弱性；提出的CLD-KB防御框架实现了高检测率与低开销的平衡，适合边缘部署。

Abstract: This paper presents an adversarial security study of the Policy-Aware LLM Retrieval-Augmented Generation (PA-LLM-RAG) framework for Internet of Battlefield Things (IoBT) mission control. We propose Query-Agnostic Semantic Retrieval Poisoning, a novel attack that injects semantically crafted rules into the IoBT knowledge base achieving high retrieval ranking across all operator query types without requiring knowledge of runtime prompts. The attack achieves 85% LLM context corruption from a single injected rule (1.6% poisoning rate) and saturates at 7.7% poisoning, demonstrating that even minimal knowledge base compromise is sufficient to corrupt mission decisions. To counter this threat, we propose CLD-KB (Cyber-Layered Defense for Knowledge Base), a dual-detector anomaly detection framework combining One-Class SVM boundary detection with a novel Member-Based Category Spread analysis that exploits the three-category IoBT policy taxonomy to identify poisoned rules before they reach the decision LLM. CLD-KB significantly outperforms five baseline methods including DBSCAN, LOF, K-Means, Isolation Forest, and One-Class SVM in both poisoning detection and knowledge preservation, with only 7ms computational overhead per mission, establishing it as an effective and edge-deployable defense for LLM-driven IoBT mission systems.

</details>


### [242] [AMRM-Pure: Semantic-Preserving Adversarial Purification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04474)
*Zhihao Dou,Zhiqiang Gao,Dongfei Cui,Weida Wang,Qinjian Zhao,Dinggen Zhang,Jun Yan,Zeke Xie,Shufei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AMRM-Pure，一种基于注意力掩码重建模型的对抗净化防御框架，通过保留图像块级语义关系来去除对抗扰动，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗净化方法多依赖扩散模型等强大生成器，聚焦于缩小对抗样本与干净样本在特征空间的差距，忽略了单个样本内部的语义相关性。本文旨在从保留图像块间语义关系的视角探索对抗净化。

Method: 1) 使用注意力掩码重建模型(AMRM)，利用其对对抗噪声的高度敏感性；2) 提出AMRM-Pure净化框架，通过保留块级语义对对抗输入去噪，将过程形式化为关于输入的可解优化问题；3) 引入分类损失微调AMRM-Pure以增强语义一致性；4) 在MAE和MaskDiT两种AMRM架构上验证。

Result: 实验证明AMRM-Pure在多个基准测试上均达到新的最先进性能。

Conclusion: 从块级语义关系角度进行对抗净化是有效的，AMRM-Pure框架充分利用了AMRM对噪声的敏感性，通过优化和微调实现鲁棒性提升。

Abstract: Adversarial purification is a defense technique that employs generative models to remove adversarial perturbations. Current methods often rely on powerful generators, typically diffusion models, and focus on reducing the gap between adversarial and clean samples in the feature space, while overlooking semantic correlation within a single sample. To address this issue, we explore adversarial purification from the perspective of preserving semantic relationships among image patches. We employ an Attentive Mask Reconstruction Model (AMRM), which shows superior performance. Our theoretical and experimental analysis reveals that AMRM is highly sensitive to adversarial noise, as such noise significantly distorts patch relationships. Based on this observation, we propose AMRM-Pure, a purification framework that denoises adversarial inputs by preserving patch-level semantics, and formulate this process as a tractable optimization problem with respect to the input. To further enhance robustness, we finetune AMRM-Pure with classification loss to strengthen semantic consistency. We apply our insight to two AMRM architectures, including Mask Autoencoder (MAE) and MaskDiT. Extensive experiments confirm the effectiveness of our method, establishing new state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks.

</details>


### [243] [Mean Time to Remediate Is Not a Fielding Model: A Cadence Audit for Enterprise Vulnerability Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04511)
*Alexander Omelchenko*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向企业漏洞管理的修复节奏审计方法，通过记录修复延迟、发布周期、紧急/常规修复比例等指标，揭示仅依赖MTTR/SLA等均值指标可能掩盖的修复节奏问题，并通过实验证明发布周期对修复容量的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有企业安全团队常用的MTTR、SLA合规、驻留时间等指标虽有用，但掩盖了修复实际到达环境的连续过程（如维护窗口、发布环、紧急旁路路径），需要一种能揭示修复节奏的审计方法。

Method: 提出修复节奏审计方法，记录常规平均延迟、发布周期、发布分数、队列几何结构、紧急/常规拆分、非现场延迟、局部剩余压力证据和声明率场景，比较连续均值简化模型与实际发布日历，计算局部容量判定和日历折扣。

Result: 在30天平均延迟假设下，两月发布列车消耗均值容量17.4%，月度发布消耗5.2%，两周筛选消耗1.3%；在16倍攻击者调整率范围内，两月折扣至少约12%，月度折扣在3-8%范围内；发布环不会自动恢复连续基准，队列错开可能有利或有害。

Conclusion: 该方法是一种可复现的治理诊断工具，当折扣相对于剩余压力不确定性或声明余量显著时，不应单独使用MTTR/SLA作为修复证据，审计将节奏评估转化为证据分辨率问题。

Abstract: Enterprise security teams commonly summarize remediation through mean time to remediate (MTTR), SLA compliance, dwell time, or detection delay. These metrics are useful, but they can hide how fixes actually reach the estate: continuously, through scheduled maintenance windows, in deployment rings, or through emergency bypass paths. This paper introduces a remediation-cadence audit for enterprise vulnerability management. The audit records routine mean lag, release period, release fraction, cohort geometry, emergency/routine split, non-fielding delay, local residual-pressure evidence, and declared rate scenario. It compares a continuous same-mean shortcut with the recorded release calendar and reports a local capacity verdict plus a calendar discount: the fraction of mean-only local capacity consumed by calendarized fielding. Worked notional packets with the same 30-day mean lag show why this matters. Under the normalized screening scenario, a two-month release train consumes 17.4\% of mean-only capacity, a monthly train 5.2\%, and a two-week screen 1.3\%; across a 16-fold attacker-adjustment rate band, the two-month discount remains at least about 12\% and the monthly discount stays in the resolution-sensitive 3--8\% range. The audit therefore turns cadence assessment into an evidence-resolution question: when the discount is material relative to residual-pressure uncertainty or claimed headroom, MTTR/SLA should not be used alone as fielding evidence. Release-geometry checks show that deployment rings do not automatically recover the continuous benchmark, and cohort staggering can help or hurt near capacity. The result is a reproducible governance diagnostic, not a breach predictor or CVE prioritizer.

</details>


### [244] [Let My Data Go: Data Brokers' Compliance with Opt-Out and Deletion Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04552)
*Elina van Kempen,Gene Tsudik,Pragya Jhunjhunwala,Mihir Raja*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对加州所有注册数据经纪商的全面研究，揭示了消费者在CCPA框架下行使数据隐私权利（选择退出、删除、获取副本）时面临的重大障碍。尽管多数经纪商表面合规，但相当一部分不回应或忽视消费者请求，部分经纪商甚至要求侵入性身份验证（CCPA明确禁止），且整个系统缺乏标准化，给消费者带来沉重负担，亟需简化流程、加强执法和严厉处罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据经纪商在消费者不知情或未同意的情况下大规模收集和出售个人信息，导致歧视、监控、身份盗窃和跟踪等危害。尽管CCPA等法规赋予消费者隐私权利，但这些权利在实践中能否有效行使尚不清楚，因此需要系统性地评估数据经纪商生态系统的实际合规情况。

Method: 使用合成消费者身份，向所有加州注册的数据经纪商提交CCPA规定的选择退出和删除请求，然后跟踪和调查各经纪商的响应行为（包括回复、确认、处理请求的情况以及身份验证要求等）。

Result: 大多数数据经纪商表面合规，但相当一部分未能回复或确认消费者的请求；部分经纪商要求进行CCPA明确禁止的侵入性消费者身份验证；各经纪商之间的请求提交流程差异巨大，给消费者带来极重的总体负担（时间和精力）。

Conclusion: 急需对消费者界面进行简化和标准化、加强执法力度，并对尤其是持续不合规的行为施加有意义的处罚。

Abstract: Data brokers are a largely American phenomenon. They collect vast amounts of personal information about most adult U.S. consumers, mainly without the latter's knowledge or consent. Accumulated data can be sold to anyone, including employers, landlords, insurance agencies, banks, governments (local, state, federal, and even foreign), as well as various malicious actors. This, in turn, enables discrimination, surveillance, identity theft, and stalking.
  Recent regulations -- such as the California Consumer Privacy Act (CCPA) modeled after EU's General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) -- were introduced to bolster consumer privacy, e.g., the rights to: (1) opt-out of the sharing or selling one's personal information, (2) delete one's personal information, and (3) obtain a copy of that information. However, exercising these rights is not easy, as shown by our comprehensive study of the data broker ecosystem. We submitted both opt-out and deletion requests (using synthetic consumer identities) under the CCPA to all California-registered data brokers and investigated their responses and lack thereof.
  While the majority seem to be compliant, a significant fraction is not and many failed to reply to (and/or acknowledge) consumer requests. Furthermore, some data brokers require intrusive consumer identity verification in order to exercise one's opt-out rights, which is explicitly disallowed by the CCPA. There is also great disparity in the request submission process among data brokers as well as an extremely heavy (time and effort) overall consumer burden. This motivates an urgent need for streamlining and standardization of the consumer interface, stronger enforcement, and meaningful consequences for (especially sustained) non-compliance.

</details>


### [245] [RustMizan: A Compilable, Contamination-Aware Benchmarking Framework for Rust Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04729)
*Tarek Elsayed,Shiping Yang,Eunsong Koh,Sanika Goyal,Vincent Huang,Paul Ngo,Nathan Young,Mohammad Omidvar Tehrani,Alvyn Kang,Arnell Kang,Zeyu Chen,Angélica Moreira,Xuan Feng,Angel X. Chang,Nick Sumner,Steven Y. Ko*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了RustMizan基准测试框架，用于评估LLM agent在Rust漏洞分析中的能力，包含可编译代码变体、多粒度标注和变异测试。实验显示前沿模型在二分类上表现尚可（56-65%），但行级定位F1仅约20%，且对抗性线索使行级F1下降约27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM漏洞分析基准存在三个不足：依赖小型不可编译代码片段、仅做二分类（有漏洞/无漏洞）、忽视了公开数据集可能已包含在模型训练语料中的风险。

Method: 构建了RustMizan框架，包含：1) crate/file/function三级的可编译代码变体；2) 二进制漏洞检测、CWE分类、函数级和行级定位的多粒度标注；3) 配对变异框架生成语义保持的代码突变体，用于污染测试和鲁棒性探测。

Result: 在四款前沿模型的agentic设置（带命令行访问）下，二分类准确率为56-65%，行级定位F1约20%，对抗性线索使行级F1下降约27%。

Conclusion: 现有前沿LLM agent在Rust漏洞分析中，粗粒度检测尚可但细粒度定位能力严重不足，且对抗性样本显著影响模型性能，表明该领域仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly applied to vulnerability analysis, but existing benchmarks have not kept pace. They typically rely on small non-compilable snippets, focus on binary classification (vulnerable or not), and do not account for the risk that publicly-released datasets are part of model training corpora. We introduce RustMizan, a benchmarking framework for Rust vulnerability analysis that addresses these gaps. RustMizan contains compilable code variants at the crate, file, and function levels, with annotations for binary vulnerability detection, CWE classification, and function- and line-level localization. A paired mutation framework produces semantics-preserving code mutants for contamination testing and robustness probing. Across four frontier models in an agentic setup with command-line access, binary classification sits in the 56-65% range, but line localization F1 stays near 20%, and adversarial cues drop line F1 by about 27%.

</details>


### [246] [HilEnT: Hilbert, Entropy Transformed Image Based Malware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04772)
*Rahul Kale,Thesath Wijayasiri,Kar Wai Fok,Vrizlynn L. L. Thing*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为HilEnT的新型恶意软件二进制到图像转换技术，结合希尔伯特曲线变换和熵特征比较，生成三通道彩色图像用于恶意软件检测，在多个数据集上取得了最先进的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件威胁日益增加，需要有效的检测和分类方法来确定响应策略。现有深度学习方法中，基于图像处理的恶意软件检测引起了研究关注，但仍有改进空间。

Method: 提出HilEnT技术：基于希尔伯特曲线的恶意软件二进制变换，结合恶意软件文件与良性/恶意类别的熵特征比较。过程中生成三张灰度图像并组合成三通道彩色图像，用于机器学习检测。进行了监督二分类和多分类实验，还评估了小样本学习方法，并研究了HOG与PCA结合的特征降维技术以提升时间性能。

Result: 在Dike、Michael Lester Dataset、Microsoft BIG 2015和自收集数据集四个数据集上进行了评估，取得了最先进的结果。

Conclusion: HilEnT技术在恶意软件检测中有效，结合图像变换和熵特征比较能提升检测性能，特征降维可改善时间效率，小样本学习验证了方法的鲁棒性。

Abstract: With the increasing threat of malware across various software related domains, malware detection and classification is critical to determine the response actions. Different strategies have been adopted to address the challenge of malware detection. With the advent of deep learning techniques, malware detection using image processing has garnered research attention. In this work, we proposed a novel malware binary to image transformation technique HilEnT based on a combination of Hilbert curve-based transformation of malware binary and the entropy feature comparison of malware file with benign and malware classes. Three grayscale images produced during this process are combined to form a three-channel colored image which is then used for malware detection using machine learning techniques. We performed supervised binary and multiclass classification to evaluate the effectiveness of our proposed HilEnT. We also evaluated a few-shot learning technique to assess the robustness of our proposed HilEnT in a practical setting where the number of available class samples is limited. Furthermore, we investigated the benefits of combination of Histogram of Oriented Gradients and Principal Component Analysis for time performance improvements through feature reduction techniques. We evaluated our proposed methodology on four datasets: Dike, Michael Lester Dataset, Microsoft BIG 2015 and a self-collected dataset, and achieved the state-of-the-art results.

</details>


### [247] [Ghost Traffic: ICMP Tunneling-Based Billing Bypass in LTE Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04783)
*Jung Jin Kim,Sungyup Nam,Seungho Jeon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Ghost Traffic系统，利用Android对ICMP流量不计费的策略漏洞，通过VpnService将应用流量封装为ICMP echo负载，实现无root权限的计费绕过攻击，在7个运营商环境中验证了6个可发生计费绕过，并提出了分层防御措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分移动ISP将ICMP echo流量视为控制流量而非用户数据，不纳入计费；同时Android默认配置允许普通应用无root权限创建ICMP echo socket。两者结合形成计费绕过漏洞，现有攻击要么需要root权限，要么缺乏端到端实现，威胁局限在少数专家手中。

Method: 提出Ghost Traffic端到端系统，利用Android VpnService将应用所有流量封装到ICMP echo负载中，无root权限通过外部代理服务器路由。提供两种变体：IPv4 ICMP隧道和IPv4-over-IPv6 ICMP隧道，分别针对公共IPv4和IPv6-only LTE环境。

Result: 在韩国、日本、美国的7个ISP环境中评估，6个环境观察到端到端隧道成功；多个环境中发生计费绕过，定量表现为数据配额耗尽后QoS限速未被触发。

Conclusion: 提出了设备、平台、网络三层级分层防御措施，进行了负责任的披露，证明不对ICMP流量计费的操作实践会导致实际的计费绕过安全威胁。

Abstract: Cellular data billing is a core operational mechanism for mobile Internet service providers (ISPs), and a policy gap that excludes a specific protocol from usage accounting can lead to a practical security threat. Some cellular ISPs treat ICMP echo traffic as control traffic rather than user data and exclude it from billing. At the same time, Android allows ordinary applications to create ICMP echo sockets without root privileges because of an unsafe default configuration, and the combination of these two conditions forms a vulnerability that can bypass data billing. Existing billing-bypass attacks either require root privileges to create raw sockets and modify routing tables, or do not provide an end-to-end implementation that works in a non-rooted environment, which limits the threat to a small group of experts. This paper proposes Ghost Traffic, an end-to-end system that uses Android's VpnService to encapsulate all application traffic into ICMP echo payloads without root privileges and route it through an external proxy server. The proposed system targets both public IPv4 environments and IPv6-only LTE environments through two variants: IPv4 ICMP tunneling and IPv4-over-IPv6 ICMP tunneling. We evaluated its applicability in seven ISP environments in South Korea, Japan, and the United States, and observed end-to-end tunneling in six of them. We observed that billing bypass occurred in multiple environments and quantitatively showed this effect by measuring that Quality of Service (QoS) throttling was not applied even after the data cap was exhausted. Finally, we propose layered countermeasures across the device, platform, and network levels, performed responsible disclosure, and show that the operational practice of not billing ICMP traffic can lead to practical billing bypass.

</details>


### [248] [Look-Ahead-Freedom as Temporal Non-Interference: A Verifiable Correctness Property for Backtesting and Agentic Trading Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04958)
*Xavier Fonseca*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明前瞻偏差（look-ahead bias）本质上是时间索引信息格上的时间非干涉性（temporal non-interference），并据此开发了一个流水线演算，区分数据的"可用时间"与"参考时间"。在值依赖情况下，前瞻自由是π₀¹-完全的（不可判定）；在值无关片段上，给出了一个线性时间可判定的类型-效应系统。实验验证了该检查器线性可扩展，能捕获差分检测和分块检测遗漏的泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前瞻偏差是回测和机器学习评估中最主要的欺骗性因素，现有方法依赖特定构造的规则和经验检测器，仅在个别通道上有效，且无法在检测无异常时提供任何保证。需要一种形式化的方法来表征和检测前瞻偏差。

Method: 将前瞻自由形式化为时间索引信息格上的时间非干涉性；提出流水线演算，分离数据的可用时间与参考时间；在值无关片段上设计线性的类型-效应系统。

Result: 前瞻偏差在值依赖情况下是π₀¹-难的（不可判定），泄漏是递归可枚举的但自由不可判定；值无关片段上的类型-效应系统在线性时间内可判定；实验表明检查器线性扩展，能捕获差分和分块检测遗漏的泄漏。

Conclusion: 前瞻自由是一个形式化属性，其检测在一般情况下不可判定，但在值无关片段上可高效实现。该理论为机器学习管道和数据流水线的前瞻偏差检测提供了严格的边界和方法。

Abstract: Look-ahead bias (using information from after a decision epoch to make the decision at that epoch) is the dominant way a backtest or a machine-learning evaluation flatters a system that will disappoint in deployment. The field manages it with construct-specific recipes and empirical detectors, which are sound only channel by channel and certify nothing by their silence. We show that look-ahead-freedom is a formal property in disguise: fixing an epoch, the demand that the future not influence the present is temporal non-interference over a time-indexed information lattice. From this identification we develop a pipeline calculus separating a datum's availability from its reference time, and settle the problem's boundary. Where availability may depend on data values, look-ahead-freedom is undecidable (indeed Pi-0-1-hard): leakage is recursively enumerable but freedom is not. On the value-independent fragment (covering windowing, resampling, joins, point-in-time and vintage reads, and agentic retrieval) we give a type-and-effect system that is sound and decidable in linear time. An artifact confirms the theory: the check scales linearly, an independent oracle witnesses no leak in any accepted pipeline, and the checker catches every planted leak that differential and tiling detectors miss.

</details>


### [249] [Measuring Healthcare Data Leaks and Security Flaws at Internet Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04965)
*Nico Brüggemann,Lukas Schmidt,Marvin Dölzer,Marius Brockhoff,Fabian Ising,Christoph Saatjohann,Sebastian Schinzel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模研究了医疗领域三大协议（HL7、FHIR、DICOM）的安全性，发现大量医疗系统存在认证缺陷、缺乏传输加密及已知软件漏洞，并披露了协调披露行动的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管医疗数据处理系统需要严格安全保护，但DICOM等协议已被证明存在安全漏洞，而广泛使用的HL7和FHIR协议的安全性却鲜少被系统性研究。作者旨在填补这一空白，首次大规模评估这三类医疗协议的安全现状。

Method: 运行低交互蜜罐9个月监测针对医疗协议的扫描行为；对IPv4和IPv6地址空间进行大规模互联网扫描，覆盖HL7、FHIR和DICOM三大协议；检测认证缺陷、TLS配置问题以及已知的医疗软件漏洞（CVE）。

Result: 发现大量存在认证缺陷的医疗服务；超过一定比例的系统不支持传输加密；相当数量的系统存在已知软件漏洞（含CVSS评分高达9.8的严重漏洞，可能导致系统被接管）。

Conclusion: 医疗部署的网络安全状况令人担忧，作者讨论了潜在原因和应对措施，并启动了协调披露活动以改善患者数据安全。

Abstract: Systems that process medical data should be meticulously secured. Yet, network services in healthcare environments often fail to implement basic security measures. For example, previous studies showed that network segmentation flaws led to DICOM systems leaking millions of patient records.
  In addition to DICOM, healthcare facilities rely heavily on the HL7 and FHIR protocols to transmit data. For nine months, we operated a low-interaction honeypot for medical protocols. We found it was regularly scanned for DICOM but never for HL7 or FHIR, indicating that despite their widespread use and importance for patient data security, the security of these services remains underexplored.
  In this paper, we present the first large-scale study on HL7 and FHIR services and expand previous work on DICOM. Our large-scale Internet scans, covering the three major healthcare protocols across IPv4 and IPv6 address spaces, identify healthcare systems and uncover data leaks due to authentication flaws. Additionally, we scanned for deficiencies in TLS configurations of these services and known insecure healthcare software.
  In total, we found \TotalEndpointsAuthFlaws ~healthcare services with authentication flaws. \NonTLSPercentage{}\% of all exposed systems do not support transport encryption, and \AllServerCVE{} systems have known software vulnerabilities, including those with potential for system takeover and CVSS scores up to 9.8. Overall, our study reveals an alarming state of cybersecurity in healthcare deployments, for which we discuss potential reasons and countermeasures. Finally, we report on the coordinated disclosure campaign we initiated to improve the security of patient data.

</details>


### [250] [TACTIC-KG: Toward Small Agent Teams for Cyber Threat Intelligence Knowledge Graph Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05001)
*Mouhamed Amine Bouchiha,Gregory Blanc*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TACTIC-KG，一种基于多智能体协作的网络安全知识图谱构建框架，通过拆解任务为多个专用轻量级LLM代理（3B-8B），在提取、类型标注、验证和策展等模块协同工作，在降低部署成本的同时，在F1分数、类型准确率和图结构相似性上优于大规模单体ICL基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的网络安全知识图谱构建方法依赖大规模单体LLM进行端到端抽取与补全，存在成本高、可控性差、性能不稳定的问题，亟需一种更加高效、可控且经济的构建方案。

Method: TACTIC-KG采用多智能体框架，将CSKG构建任务分解为抽取、类型标注、验证和策展四个模块化步骤，每个步骤由专用的轻量级LLM代理（3B-8B参数规模）负责执行。

Result: 在人工标注的CTI报告上，TACTIC-KG的代理专业化方法在抽取F1分数、类型准确率和结构图相似性三个指标上持续优于更大规模的单体上下文学习（ICL）基线。

Conclusion: 将复杂的CSKG构建任务分解为多个专用轻量级LLM代理协同工作，比使用单一大规模LLM更稳定、有效且经济，为提高网络安全威胁情报自动化分析能力提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Cyber Threat Intelligence (CTI) reports are predominantly unstructured, heterogeneous, and noisy, which limits their direct usability for automated analysis and reasoning. Cybersecurity Knowledge Graphs (CSKGs) provide a structured representation of adversarial entities, actions, and relations, but constructing such graphs from free-text CTI remains a challenge. Recent approaches rely on monolithic Large Language Models (LLMs) to perform end-to-end extraction and completion, leading to high cost, limited controllability, and unstable performance. This paper introduces TACTIC-KG, an agentic framework for CSKG construction that decomposes the task into modular, specialized LLM agents responsible for extraction, typing, verification, and curation. Using lightweight models (3B--8B), TACTIC-KG improves stability, recall, and graph consistency while reducing deployment cost. We implement and evaluate TACTIC-KG against recent state-of-the-art systems. Experiments on human-annotated CTI reports show that agent specialization consistently outperforms larger monolithic in-context-learning (ICL) baselines in extraction F1-score, typing accuracy, and structural graph similarity.

</details>


### [251] [Algorithmically Presented Numbers and Canonical Representations in Cryptographic Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05016)
*Arslan Brömme*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文从表示论视角审视密码协议，区分了"算法可逼近值"（可计算实数）与"系统内有限精确可描述值"（A_fin(S)），证明了不存在可计算的扩展规范器将任意可计算实数的逼近程序统一转换为唯一有限值编码。以有理数系统及其规范编码规范Σ_Q作为操作理性核心表示，并将"规范可序列化对象类"的概念扩展至实际协议对象（文件、哈希值、交易ID等），通过对称/非对称加密、哈希及区块链快照根哈希锚定协议等实例，阐明值的数学确定性与协议对象的操作唯一性是两种不同要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密码协议分析多关注值的可计算性（外延属性），但忽略了表示系统中值的呈现算法结构对协议操作的关键影响——协议中不仅需要算法可访问的值，其固定表示方式同样具有决定性作用。

Method: 定义了三种表示论概念（A_app、A_fin(S)、规范可归一化），通过不可计算性证明建立理论框架；以有理数系统Σ_Q为核心模型，将规范可序列化概念推广至实际协议对象，并通过加密、哈希和区块链协议实例进行验证。

Result: 证明了不存在可计算的扩展规范器能将可计算实数统一转为唯一有限编码；展示了规范表示规范固定后，字节级正确性和良定义性论证可独立于实现相关的序列化或舍入决策。

Conclusion: 值的数学确定性与协议对象的操作唯一性是两种不同的要求；规范化的表示规范是密码协议互操作性、良定义性和形式化验证的关键前提。

Abstract: This paper develops a representation-theoretic perspective on cryptographic protocols. The focus is not solely on the computability of the abstract value as an extensional property, but on the algorithmic structure of its presentation in a representation system: for operational use in protocols, algorithmic accessibility of the value does not suffice; its fixed presentation is also decisive. We distinguish three representation-theoretic notions -- algorithmically approximable (A_app, the computable real numbers), finitely exactly describable in a system (A_fin(S)), and canonical normalizability of a system -- and show that there is no computable extensional canonicalizer that uniformly transforms arbitrary approximation programs of computable real numbers into unique finite value encodings. As the operational rational core presentation we use the rational system with its canonical encoding specification Sigma_Q (fixed rules for valid fraction descriptions, canonical codes, and normalization); the associated value set is A_ex = Q. The notion of a canonically serializable object class transfers this core idea to practical protocol objects (files as byte sequences, hash values, transaction IDs, and normatively serialized payloads). We illustrate the consequences for interoperability, well-definedness, and verification with fully worked toy examples from symmetric and asymmetric encryption and hashing, and with a real-world example, the snaproot hash-anchoring protocol for blockchain-based file integrity verification. The paper thereby shows that the mathematical determinacy of a value and its operational uniqueness as a protocol object are two different requirements.
